03954:     function getPersonnelServiceDefinition(serviceId = PERSONNEL_STATE.service) {
03955:         return PERSONNEL_SERVICE_DEFINITIONS[serviceId]
03956:             || PERSONNEL_SERVICE_DEFINITIONS.medical;

03959:     function getPersonnelProfileDefinition(serviceId = PERSONNEL_STATE.service, profileId = PERSONNEL_STATE.profile) {
03960:         const service = getPersonnelServiceDefinition(serviceId);
03961:         return service.profiles.find(profile => profile.id === profileId)
03962:             || service.profiles[0];
03963:     }

03965:     function isLivePersonnelProfile() {
03966:         return getPersonnelProfileDefinition()?.live === true;
03967:     }

03969:     function normalisePersonnelUnitsRequired(value) {
03970:         const parsed = Number.parseInt(String(value ?? '').trim(), 10);
03971:         if (!Number.isFinite(parsed) || parsed < 0) return 0;
03972:         return Math.min(9999, parsed);
03973:     }
03974: 
03975:     function isPersonnelPerStationTargetMode() {
03976:         return (
03977:             PERSONNEL_STATE.mode === 'target'
03978:             && PERSONNEL_STATE.unitsRequired > 0
03979:         );
03980:     }
03981: 
03982:     function getPersonnelUnitsRemaining() {
03983:         return isPersonnelPerStationTargetMode()
03984:             ? PERSONNEL_STATE.unitsRequired
03985:             : Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
03986:     }

03989:     function formatPersonnelUnitsFulfilled() {
03990:         if (isPersonnelPerStationTargetMode()) {
03991:             const target = PERSONNEL_STATE.unitsRequired;
03992:             const latestFulfilled = PERSONNEL_STATE.lastStationReport
03993:                 && !PERSONNEL_STATE.lastStationReport.error
03994:                     ? Math.min(
03995:                         target,
03996:                         Number(
03997:                             PERSONNEL_STATE.lastStationReport.fulfilledUnits
03998:                             || 0
03999:                         )
04000:                     )
04001:                     : 0;
04002: 
04003:             return `${PERSONNEL_STATE.stationsTargetMet} / ${PERSONNEL_STATE.completedStations} stations met | latest ${latestFulfilled}/${target}`;
04004:         }
04005: 
04006:         return `${PERSONNEL_STATE.unitsFulfilled} / All`;
04007:     }

04310:     function updatePersonnelProfileUi(save = true) {
04311:         const service = getPersonnelServiceDefinition();
04312:         const profile = getPersonnelProfileDefinition();
04313:         const live = isLivePersonnelProfile();
04314: 
04315:         const training = document.querySelector('#mc-personnel-profile-training');
04316:         const vehicle = document.querySelector('#mc-personnel-profile-vehicle');
04317:         const requirement = document.querySelector('#mc-personnel-profile-requirement');
04318:         const policy = document.querySelector('#mc-personnel-policy-summary');
04319:         const banner = document.querySelector('#mc-personnel-profile-banner');
04320:         const modeAll = document.querySelector('#mc-personnel-mode-all-option');
04321:         const start = document.querySelector('#mc-personnel-start');
04322:         const refresh = document.querySelector('#mc-personnel-refresh');
04323:         const mode = document.querySelector('#mc-personnel-mode');
04324:         const action = document.querySelector('#mc-personnel-action');
04325:         const unitsRequired = document.querySelector('#mc-personnel-units-required');
04326:         const startFrom = document.querySelector('#mc-personnel-startfrom');
04327: 
04328:         if (training) training.textContent = profile.label;
04329:         if (vehicle) vehicle.textContent = profile.vehicle;
04330:         if (requirement) requirement.textContent = profile.requirement;
04331:         if (policy) {
04332:             policy.innerHTML = `<strong>${service.icon} ${service.label} policy:</strong> ${profile.policy}`;
04333:         }
04334: 
04335:         if (banner) {
04336:             banner.classList.toggle('is-live', live);
04337:             banner.classList.toggle('is-preview', !live);
04338:             banner.textContent = live
04339:                 ? '● LIVE PROFILE — the selected assignment engine is enabled.'
04340:                 : '◐ UI PREVIEW ONLY — assignment controls are safely disabled for this profile.';
04341:         }
04342: 
04343:         if (modeAll) {
04344:             modeAll.textContent = live
04345:                 ? `All ${getPersonnelStationScopeLabel()} from selected start point`
04346:                 : `All relevant ${service.label} stations from selected start point`;
04347:         }
04348: 
04349:         [start, refresh, mode, action, startFrom].forEach(control => {
04350:             if (control) control.disabled = !live;
04351:         });
04352: 
04353:         if (unitsRequired) {
04354:             unitsRequired.disabled =
04355:                 !live ||
04356:                 (
04357:                     document.querySelector(
04358:                         '#mc-personnel-mode'
04359:                     )?.value ||
04360:                     'all'
04361:                 ) !== 'target';
04362:         }
04363: 
04364:         if (start) {
04365:             start.title = live
04366:                 ? 'Start the selected Personnel Assignment profile'
04367:                 : 'This profile is a UI preview and cannot make assignments yet';
04368:         }
04369: 
04370:         if (live) {
04371:             setPersonnelUiValue('status', PERSONNEL_STATE.filteredStations.length ? 'Ready' : 'Refresh required');
04372:         } else {
04373:             setPersonnelUiValue('status', 'Profile UI preview');
04374:             setPersonnelUiValue('vehicle', 'Not enabled');
04375:         }
04376: 
04377:         if (save) {
04378:             PERSONNEL_STATE.stations = [];
04379:             PERSONNEL_STATE.filteredStations = [];
04380:             PERSONNEL_STATE.queue = [];
04381:             PERSONNEL_STATE.selectedHref = '';
04382:             PERSONNEL_STATE.unitsFulfilled = 0;
04383:             PERSONNEL_STATE.unitTargetReached = false;
04384:             setPersonnelUiValue('unitsFulfilled', PERSONNEL_STATE.unitsRequired > 0 ? `0 / ${PERSONNEL_STATE.unitsRequired}` : '0 / All');
04385:             populatePersonnelStartDropdown();
04386:             setPersonnelUiValue('status', live ? 'Refresh required' : 'Profile UI preview');
04387:             setPersonnelUiValue('vehicle', live ? 'None' : 'Not enabled');
04388: 
04389:             try {
04390:                 localStorage.setItem(PERSONNEL_SERVICE_STORAGE_KEY, PERSONNEL_STATE.service);
04391:                 localStorage.setItem(PERSONNEL_PROFILE_STORAGE_KEY, PERSONNEL_STATE.profile);
04392:             } catch (_error) {}
04393: 
04394:             personnelLog(`${service.icon} Service: ${service.label} | Training profile: ${profile.label}${live ? ' (LIVE)' : ' (UI preview only)'}`, live ? 'info' : 'debug');
04395:         }
04396: 
04397:         updatePersonnelModeUi();
04398:     }

04400:     function initialisePersonnelReportVisibility() {
04401:         const stationCheckbox = document.querySelector('#mc-personnel-show-station-report');
04402:         const overallCheckbox = document.querySelector('#mc-personnel-show-overall-report');
04403:         if (!stationCheckbox || !overallCheckbox) return;
04404: 
04405:         let showStation = true;
04406:         let showOverall = true;
04407: 
04408:         try {
04409:             showStation = localStorage.getItem(PERSONNEL_STATION_REPORT_VISIBLE_KEY) !== 'false';
04410:             showOverall = localStorage.getItem(PERSONNEL_OVERALL_REPORT_VISIBLE_KEY) !== 'false';
04411:         } catch (_error) {}
04412: 
04413:         stationCheckbox.checked = showStation;
04414:         overallCheckbox.checked = showOverall;
04415:         updatePersonnelReportVisibility(false);
04416:     }
04417: 
04418:     function updatePersonnelReportVisibility(save = true) {
04419:         const stationCheckbox = document.querySelector('#mc-personnel-show-station-report');
04420:         const overallCheckbox = document.querySelector('#mc-personnel-show-overall-report');
04421:         const stationSection = document.querySelector('#mc-personnel-after-action-section');
04422:         const overallSection = document.querySelector('#mc-personnel-overall-report-section');
04423: 
04424:         if (!stationCheckbox || !overallCheckbox || !stationSection || !overallSection) return;
04425: 
04426:         const showStation = Boolean(stationCheckbox.checked);
04427:         const showOverall = Boolean(overallCheckbox.checked);
04428: 
04429:         stationSection.hidden = !showStation;
04430:         overallSection.hidden = !showOverall;
04431:         stationCheckbox.setAttribute('aria-expanded', showStation ? 'true' : 'false');
04432:         overallCheckbox.setAttribute('aria-expanded', showOverall ? 'true' : 'false');
04433: 
04434:         if (save) {
04435:             try {
04436:                 localStorage.setItem(PERSONNEL_STATION_REPORT_VISIBLE_KEY, showStation ? 'true' : 'false');
04437:                 localStorage.setItem(PERSONNEL_OVERALL_REPORT_VISIBLE_KEY, showOverall ? 'true' : 'false');
04438:             } catch (_error) {}
04439:         }
04440:     }

04459:     function setPanelCollapsed(collapsed, save = true) {
04460:         const panel = document.querySelector('#mc-namer-panel');
04461:         const button = document.querySelector('#mc-namer-collapse');
04462:         if (!panel || !button) return;
04463: 
04464:         panel.classList.toggle('mc-namer-collapsed', Boolean(collapsed));
04465:         button.textContent = collapsed ? '+' : '−';
04466:         button.setAttribute('aria-expanded', collapsed ? 'false' : 'true');
04467:         button.title = collapsed ? 'Expand naming tools' : 'Collapse naming tools';
04468: 
04469:         if (save) {
04470:             try {
04471:                 localStorage.setItem(COLLAPSED_STORAGE_KEY, collapsed ? 'true' : 'false');
04472:             } catch (e) {}
04473:         }
04474:     }

04476:     function switchToolTab(tabName, force = false) {
04477:         const targetTab = ['unit', 'station', 'personnel'].includes(tabName)
04478:             ? tabName
04479:             : 'unit';
04480:         const panel = document.querySelector('#mc-namer-panel');
04481:         const unitView = document.querySelector('#mc-unit-view');
04482:         const stationView = document.querySelector('#mc-station-view');
04483:         const personnelView = document.querySelector('#mc-personnel-view');
04484:         const unitTab = document.querySelector('#mc-tab-unit');
04485:         const stationTab = document.querySelector('#mc-tab-station');
04486:         const personnelTab = document.querySelector('#mc-tab-personnel');
04487:         const headerTitle = document.querySelector('#mc-namer-header-title');
04488:         const headerVersion = document.querySelector('#mc-namer-header-version');
04489: 
04490:         if (
04491:             !panel ||
04492:             !unitView ||
04493:             !stationView ||
04494:             !personnelView ||
04495:             !unitTab ||
04496:             !stationTab ||
04497:             !personnelTab ||
04498:             !headerTitle ||
04499:             !headerVersion
04500:         ) {
04501:             return;
04502:         }
04503: 
04504:         if (!force && STATE.running && targetTab !== 'unit') {
04505:             log('Stop the Unit Naming Tool before switching tabs.', 'error');
04506:             return;
04507:         }
04508: 
04509:         if (!force && STATION_STATE.running && targetTab !== 'station') {
04510:             stationLog('Stop the Station Naming Tool before switching tabs.', 'error');
04511:             return;
04512:         }
04513: 
04514:         if (!force && PERSONNEL_STATE.running && targetTab !== 'personnel') {
04515:             personnelLog('Stop Personnel Assignment before switching tabs.', 'error');
04516:             return;
04517:         }
04518: 
04519:         const showUnit = targetTab === 'unit';
04520:         const showStation = targetTab === 'station';
04521:         const showPersonnel = targetTab === 'personnel';
04522: 
04523:         unitView.style.display = showUnit ? 'grid' : 'none';
04524:         stationView.style.display = showStation ? 'grid' : 'none';
04525:         personnelView.style.display = showPersonnel ? 'grid' : 'none';
04526: 
04527:         unitTab.classList.toggle('active', showUnit);
04528:         stationTab.classList.toggle('active', showStation);
04529:         personnelTab.classList.toggle('active', showPersonnel);
04530:         unitTab.setAttribute('aria-selected', showUnit ? 'true' : 'false');
04531:         stationTab.setAttribute('aria-selected', showStation ? 'true' : 'false');
04532:         personnelTab.setAttribute('aria-selected', showPersonnel ? 'true' : 'false');
04533: 
04534:         headerTitle.textContent = showUnit
04535:             ? 'Unit Naming'
04536:             : showStation
04537:                 ? 'Station Naming'
04538:                 : 'Personnel Assignment';
04539:         headerVersion.textContent = showUnit
04540:             ? `v${UNIT_VERSION}`
04541:             : showStation
04542:                 ? `v${STATION_VERSION}`
04543:                 : `v${PERSONNEL_VERSION}`;
04544:         panel.dataset.activeTool = targetTab;
04545: 
04546:         try {
04547:             localStorage.setItem(ACTIVE_TAB_STORAGE_KEY, targetTab);
04548:         } catch (_error) {}
04549: 
04550:         requestAnimationFrame(() => {
04551:             clampToolPanelToViewport(panel);
04552:         });
04553:     }
04554: 
04555:     function refreshStationNamingStations() {
04556:         if (PERSONNEL_STATE.running) {
04557:             stationLog('Personnel Assignment is currently running. Stop it before refreshing Station Naming.', 'error');
04558:             setStationUiValue('status', 'Blocked by Personnel Assignment');
04559:             return;
04560:         }
04561: 
04562:         if (STATE.running) {
04563:             stationLog('Unit Naming is currently running. Stop it before refreshing Station Naming.', 'error');
04564:             setStationUiValue('status', 'Blocked by Unit Naming');
04565:             return;
04566:         }
04567: 
04568:         if (STATION_STATE.running) {
04569:             stationLog('Stop Station Naming before refreshing the station list.', 'error');
04570:             return;
04571:         }
04572: 
04573:         setStationUiValue('status', 'Refreshing stations...');
04574:         stationLog('Refreshing station list and reading MissionChief building type IDs...', 'info');
04575: 
04576:         const stationEntries = getStationOverviewEntries();
04577: 
04578:         STATION_STATE.stations = stationEntries
04579:             .map((entry, index) => {
04580:                 const typeInfo = getStationNamingTypeInfo(
04581:                     entry.buildingTypeId,
04582:                     entry.displayName
04583:                 );
04584: 
04585:                 return {
04586:                     index,
04587:                     href: entry.href,
04588:                     buildingId: entry.buildingId,
04589:                     displayName: entry.displayName,
04590:                     buildingTypeId: entry.buildingTypeId,
04591:                     stationType: typeInfo.stationType,
04592:                     suffix: typeInfo.suffix,
04593:                     typeLabel: typeInfo.label
04594:                 };
04595:             })
04596:             .filter(station => station.href && station.buildingId);
04597: 
04598:         populateStationNamingStartDropdown();
04599:         setStationUiValue('status', 'Ready');
04600: 
04601:         stationLog(`Stations found: ${STATION_STATE.stations.length}`, 'info');
04602:         stationLog(`Matching filter: ${STATION_STATE.filteredStations.length}`, 'info');
04603: 
04604:         if (STATION_STATE.filteredStations.length) {
04605:             stationLog(`First match: ${STATION_STATE.filteredStations[0].displayName}`, 'info');
04606:             stationLog(`Last match : ${STATION_STATE.filteredStations[STATION_STATE.filteredStations.length - 1].displayName}`, 'info');
04607:         }
04608:     }
04609: 
04610:     function getStationNamingTypeInfo(buildingTypeId, displayName) {
04611:         const name = cleanText(displayName).toUpperCase();
04612: 
04613:         // Airport fire stations share MissionChief's normal fire-station type IDs.
04614:         // Use an explicit existing name marker only for this special case.
04615:         if ((buildingTypeId === 0 || buildingTypeId === 18)
04616:             && (/\bAIRPORT\b|\bAIRFIELD\b/.test(name) || /-AF\d*$/.test(name))) {
04617:             return { stationType: 'AIRFIELD', suffix: '-AF', label: 'Airfield station' };
04618:         }
04619: 
04620:         const mapped = STATION_BUILDING_TYPE_INFO[buildingTypeId];
04621:         if (mapped) return mapped;
04622: 
04623:         const detectedType = detectStationType(displayName);
04624:         const fallbackByType = {
04625:             FIRE: { stationType: 'FIRE', suffix: '-FS', label: 'Fire station' },
04626:             AMBULANCE: { stationType: 'AMBULANCE', suffix: '-AS', label: 'Ambulance station' },
04627:             POLICE: { stationType: 'POLICE', suffix: '-PS', label: 'Police station' },
04628:             RNLI: { stationType: 'RNLI', suffix: '-RNLI', label: 'RNLI / lifeboat station' },
04629:             COASTGUARD: { stationType: 'COASTGUARD', suffix: '-CG', label: 'Coastguard station' },
04630:             SAR: { stationType: 'SAR', suffix: '-SAR', label: 'Search and rescue station' },
04631:             AIRFIELD: { stationType: 'AIRFIELD', suffix: '-AF', label: 'Airfield station' },
04632:             EOD: { stationType: 'EOD', suffix: '-EOD', label: 'EOD / bomb disposal station' },
04633:             RECOVERY: { stationType: 'RECOVERY', suffix: '-REC', label: 'Recovery station' }
04634:         };
04635: 
04636:         if (detectedType === 'AIR') {
04637:             return /POLICE/i.test(name)
04638:                 ? { stationType: 'AIR', suffix: '-PH', label: 'Police helicopter station' }
04639:                 : { stationType: 'AIR', suffix: '-AA', label: 'Air ambulance station' };
04640:         }
04641: 
04642:         return fallbackByType[detectedType]
04643:             || { stationType: 'OTHER', suffix: '', label: `Unsupported building type ${buildingTypeId ?? 'unknown'}` };
04644:     }

04654:         });

04700:     function startStationNamingRun() {
04701:         if (PERSONNEL_STATE.running) {
04702:             stationLog('Personnel Assignment is currently running. Stop it before starting Station Naming.', 'error');
04703:             setStationUiValue('status', 'Blocked by Personnel Assignment');
04704:             return;
04705:         }
04706: 
04707:         if (STATE.running) {
04708:             stationLog('Unit Naming is currently running. Stop it before starting Station Naming.', 'error');
04709:             setStationUiValue('status', 'Blocked by Unit Naming');
04710:             return;
04711:         }
04712: 
04713:         if (STATION_STATE.running) {
04714:             stationLog('Station Naming is already running.', 'debug');
04715:             return;
04716:         }
04717: 
04718:         if (!STATION_STATE.filteredStations.length) {
04719:             stationLog('No matching stations loaded. Press Refresh Stations first.', 'error');
04720:             setStationUiValue('status', 'Refresh required');
04721:             return;
04722:         }
04723: 
04724:         const selectedHref = document.querySelector('#mc-station-startfrom')?.value || '';
04725:         const startIndex = STATION_STATE.filteredStations.findIndex(station => station.href === selectedHref);
04726: 
04727:         if (startIndex < 0) {
04728:             stationLog(`Selected start station not found: ${selectedHref || 'none'}`, 'error');
04729:             setStationUiValue('status', 'Invalid start station');
04730:             return;
04731:         }
04732: 
04733:         STATION_STATE.mode = document.querySelector('#mc-station-mode')?.value || 'all';
04734:         STATION_STATE.action = document.querySelector('#mc-station-action')?.value || 'preview';
04735:         STATION_STATE.queue = STATION_STATE.mode === 'single'
04736:             ? [STATION_STATE.filteredStations[startIndex]]
04737:             : STATION_STATE.filteredStations.slice(startIndex);
04738:         STATION_STATE.running = true;
04739:         STATION_STATE.paused = false;
04740:         STATION_STATE.stopped = false;
04741:         STATION_STATE.stationIndex = 0;
04742:         STATION_STATE.renamedCount = 0;
04743:         STATION_STATE.skippedCount = 0;
04744:         STATION_STATE.previewedCount = 0;
04745:         STATION_STATE.activeIframe = null;
04746: 
04747:         document.querySelector('#mc-station-pause').textContent = 'Pause';
04748:         updateStationCounters();
04749:         setStationUiValue('progress', `0 / ${STATION_STATE.queue.length}`);
04750:         setStationUiValue('status', STATION_STATE.action === 'save' ? 'Starting rename run' : 'Starting preview');
04751: 
04752:         stationLog(`Mode: ${STATION_STATE.mode === 'single' ? 'Selected station only' : 'All matching stations from selected start point'}`, 'info');
04753:         stationLog(`Action: ${STATION_STATE.action === 'save' ? 'RENAME AND SAVE' : 'PREVIEW ONLY - no names will be saved'}`, STATION_STATE.action === 'save' ? 'after' : 'info');
04754:         stationLog(`Starting from: ${STATION_STATE.queue[0]?.displayName || 'None'}`, 'info');
04755: 
04756:         processStationNamingQueue().catch(error => {
04757:             stationLog(`Station Naming stopped after an unexpected error: ${error?.message || error}`, 'error');
04758:             stationDebug(error?.stack || String(error));
04759:             STATION_STATE.stopped = true;
04760:             STATION_STATE.running = false;
04761:             setStationUiValue('status', 'Error');
04762:         });
04763:     }

04765:     function toggleStationNamingPause() {
04766:         if (!STATION_STATE.running) {
04767:             stationLog('Nothing is currently running.', 'debug');
04768:             return;
04769:         }
04770: 
04771:         STATION_STATE.paused = !STATION_STATE.paused;
04772:         document.querySelector('#mc-station-pause').textContent = STATION_STATE.paused ? 'Resume' : 'Pause';
04773:         setStationUiValue('status', STATION_STATE.paused ? 'Paused' : 'Running');
04774:         stationLog(STATION_STATE.paused ? 'Paused. The current safe step will finish before waiting.' : 'Resumed.', 'debug');
04775:     }
04776: 
04777:     function stopStationNamingRun() {
04778:         if (!STATION_STATE.running) {
04779:             stationLog('Nothing is currently running.', 'debug');
04780:             return;
04781:         }
04782: 
04783:         STATION_STATE.stopped = true;
04784:         STATION_STATE.paused = false;
04785:         document.querySelector('#mc-station-pause').textContent = 'Pause';
04786:         setStationUiValue('status', 'Stopping...');
04787: 
04788:         for (const controller of STATION_STATE.activeControllers) {
04789:             try {
04790:                 controller.abort();
04791:             } catch (_error) {}
04792:         }
04793:         STATION_STATE.activeControllers.clear();
04794: 
04795:         stationLog('Stop requested. Active station requests were cancelled and no further station will be saved.', 'error');
04796:     }
04797: 
04798:     async function processStationNamingQueue() {
04799:         try {
04800:             while (STATION_STATE.stationIndex < STATION_STATE.queue.length) {
04801:                 if (STATION_STATE.stopped) break;
04802: 
04803:                 await waitIfStationNamingPaused();
04804:                 if (STATION_STATE.stopped) break;
04805: 
04806:                 const station = STATION_STATE.queue[STATION_STATE.stationIndex];
04807:                 const displayPosition = STATION_STATE.stationIndex + 1;
04808: 
04809:                 setStationUiValue('progress', `${displayPosition} / ${STATION_STATE.queue.length}`);
04810:                 setStationUiValue('current', station.displayName);
04811:                 setStationUiValue('address', 'Opening station...');
04812:                 setStationUiValue('town', 'Waiting...');
04813:                 setStationUiValue('before', station.displayName);
04814:                 setStationUiValue('after', 'Waiting...');
04815:                 setStationUiValue('status', 'Opening station');
04816: 
04817:                 stationLog(`Station ${displayPosition}/${STATION_STATE.queue.length}: ${station.displayName}`, 'station');
04818:                 stationLog(`Type: ${station.typeLabel} | building_type_id=${station.buildingTypeId ?? 'unknown'} | suffix=${station.suffix || 'none'}`, 'info');
04819: 
04820:                 const result = await processOneStationName(station);
04821: 
04822:                 if (result === 'renamed') {
04823:                     STATION_STATE.renamedCount++;
04824:                 } else if (result === 'previewed') {
04825:                     STATION_STATE.previewedCount++;
04826:                 } else {
04827:                     STATION_STATE.skippedCount++;
04828:                 }
04829: 
04830:                 updateStationCounters();
04831:                 STATION_STATE.stationIndex++;
04832: 
04833:                 if (!STATION_STATE.stopped) await sleep(350);
04834:             }
04835:         } finally {
04836:             await closeStationNamingModal();
04837:             STATION_STATE.activeIframe = null;
04838:             STATION_STATE.running = false;
04839:             STATION_STATE.paused = false;
04840:             document.querySelector('#mc-station-pause').textContent = 'Pause';
04841: 
04842:             if (STATION_STATE.stopped) {
04843:                 setStationUiValue('status', 'Stopped');
04844:                 stationLog(`Stopped. Renamed ${STATION_STATE.renamedCount}, previewed ${STATION_STATE.previewedCount}, skipped ${STATION_STATE.skippedCount}.`, 'error');
04845:             } else {
04846:                 setStationUiValue('status', 'Finished');
04847:                 setStationUiValue('progress', `${STATION_STATE.queue.length} / ${STATION_STATE.queue.length}`);
04848:                 stationLog(`Finished. Renamed ${STATION_STATE.renamedCount}, previewed ${STATION_STATE.previewedCount}, skipped ${STATION_STATE.skippedCount}.`, 'done');
04849:             }
04850:         }
04851:     }
04852: 
04853:     async function processOneStationName(station) {
04854:         if (!station.suffix) {
04855:             stationLog(`Skipped: no safe naming rule exists for building_type_id=${station.buildingTypeId ?? 'unknown'}.`, 'error');
04856:             return 'skipped';
04857:         }
04858: 
04859:         const stationEntry = findStationOverviewEntry(station.href);
04860:         const stationLink = stationEntry?.link || null;
04861:         if (!stationLink) {
04862:             stationLog(`Skipped: station link no longer exists for ${station.href}.`, 'error');
04863:             return 'skipped';
04864:         }
04865: 
04866:         let iframe = null;
04867: 
04868:         try {
04869:             iframe = await openStationWorkflowIframe(
04870:                 stationEntry,
04871:                 'station-naming',
04872:                 waitForStationNamingIframe
04873:             );
04874:             STATION_STATE.activeIframe = iframe;
04875: 
04876:             if (!iframe) throw new Error('Station lightbox iframe did not open.');
04877: 
04878:             let doc = await waitForStationNamingDocument(
04879:                 iframe,
04880:                 currentDoc => currentDoc.querySelector(`a[href="${station.href}/edit"]`),
04881:                 'normal station page'
04882:             );
04883: 
04884:             if (!doc) throw new Error('Normal station page did not finish loading.');
04885:             if (STATION_STATE.stopped) return 'skipped';
04886: 
04887:             await waitIfStationNamingPaused();
04888:             setStationUiValue('status', 'Reading station address');
04889: 
04890:             // Do not depend on clicking Move building. MissionChief can block that page
04891:             // while vehicles are away. First try a read-only background request; when
04892:             // that is unavailable, use the station coordinates and MissionChief's own
04893:             // /reverse_address endpoint. The Move form is never submitted.
04894:             const addressResult = await resolveStationAddress(station, doc);
04895:             const address = String(addressResult.address || '').trim();
04896: 
04897:             if (!address) {
04898:                 throw new Error(addressResult.error || 'Station address could not be read from the Move page or coordinate fallback.');
04899:             }
04900: 
04901:             setStationUiValue('address', address);
04902:             stationLog(`Address: ${address}`, 'info');
04903:             stationLog(`Address source: ${addressResult.source}`, 'debug');
04904: 
04905:             const parsed = extractStationArea(address);
04906:             if (!parsed.postcode) throw new Error('No UK postcode could be extracted from the address.');
04907:             if (!parsed.area) throw new Error(`No local area was found after postcode ${parsed.postcode}.`);
04908: 
04909:             const formattedArea = formatStationArea(parsed.area);
04910:             if (!formattedArea) throw new Error('The detected local area became empty after formatting.');
04911: 
04912:             setStationUiValue('town', formattedArea);
04913:             stationLog(`Postcode: ${parsed.postcode}`, 'info');
04914:             stationLog(`Detected area: ${formattedArea}`, 'after');
04915: 
04916:             let proposedName = buildStationName(formattedArea, station.suffix, station.displayName);
04917:             setStationUiValue('after', proposedName);
04918:             stationLog(`BEFORE: ${station.displayName}`, 'before');
04919:             stationLog(`AFTER : ${proposedName}`, 'after');
04920: 
04921:             if (STATION_STATE.action !== 'save') {
04922:                 setStationUiValue('status', 'Preview complete');
04923:                 stationLog('Preview only: nothing was saved.', 'info');
04924:                 return 'previewed';
04925:             }
04926: 
04927:             if (STATION_STATE.stopped) return 'skipped';
04928:             await waitIfStationNamingPaused();
04929: 
04930:             // The iframe is still on the normal station page because address reading
04931:             // now happens in the background. Open Edit directly from this page.
04932:             if (STATION_STATE.stopped) return 'skipped';
04933: 
04934:             setStationUiValue('status', 'Opening Edit');
04935:             const editLink = doc.querySelector(`a[href="${station.href}/edit"]`);
04936:             if (!editLink) throw new Error('Edit button was not found.');
04937: 
04938:             stationDebug(`Clicking Edit: ${editLink.getAttribute('href')}`);
04939:             editLink.click();
04940: 
04941:             doc = await waitForStationNamingDocument(
04942:                 iframe,
04943:                 currentDoc => currentDoc.querySelector('#building_name')
04944:                     && currentDoc.querySelector(`form[action="${station.href}"] input[name="_method"][value="patch"]`),
04945:                 'Edit station page'
04946:             );
04947: 
04948:             if (!doc) throw new Error('Edit station page or #building_name did not load.');
04949: 
04950:             const nameInput = doc.querySelector('#building_name');
04951:             const saveButton = doc.querySelector('input.btn-success[type="submit"][value="Save"], button.btn-success[type="submit"]');
04952:             if (!nameInput || !saveButton) throw new Error('Station name field or Save button was not found.');
04953: 
04954:             const actualBefore = String(nameInput.value || '').trim();
04955:             proposedName = buildStationName(formattedArea, station.suffix, actualBefore);
04956:             setStationUiValue('before', actualBefore || station.displayName);
04957:             setStationUiValue('after', proposedName);
04958: 
04959:             if (actualBefore === proposedName) {
04960:                 stationLog(`Already correct: ${proposedName}`, 'info');
04961:                 setStationUiValue('status', 'Already correct');
04962:                 return 'skipped';
04963:             }
04964: 
04965:             if (STATION_STATE.stopped) return 'skipped';
04966:             await waitIfStationNamingPaused();
04967: 
04968:             nameInput.focus();
04969:             nameInput.value = proposedName;
04970:             nameInput.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
04971:             nameInput.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
04972: 
04973:             stationLog(`EDIT BEFORE: ${actualBefore}`, 'before');
04974:             stationLog(`EDIT AFTER : ${proposedName}`, 'after');
04975:             setStationUiValue('status', 'Saving station');
04976: 
04977:             saveButton.click();
04978: 
04979:             const saved = await verifyStationNameSaved(station.href, proposedName);
04980:             if (!saved) throw new Error('Save could not be verified. The script did not count this station as renamed.');
04981: 
04982:             station.displayName = proposedName;
04983:             updateStationListName(station.href, proposedName);
04984:             setStationUiValue('status', 'Saved');
04985:             stationLog(`Saved and verified: ${proposedName}`, 'done');
04986:             return 'renamed';
04987:         } catch (error) {
04988:             stationLog(`Skipped ${station.displayName}: ${error?.message || error}`, 'error');
04989:             stationDebug(error?.stack || String(error));
04990:             return 'skipped';
04991:         } finally {
04992:             await closeStationNamingModal();
04993:             STATION_STATE.activeIframe = null;
04994:         }
04995:     }
04996: 
04997:     async function resolveStationAddress(station, stationDoc) {
04998:         const movePageResult = await fetchStationMoveAddress(station);
04999:         if (movePageResult.address) return movePageResult;
05000: 
05001:         stationDebug(`Move-page address unavailable for ${station.displayName}: ${movePageResult.error || 'no #building_address returned'}`);
05002: 
05003:         const coordinates = await getStationCoordinates(station, stationDoc);
05004:         if (!coordinates) {
05005:             return {
05006:                 address: '',
05007:                 source: 'none',
05008:                 error: 'Move building is unavailable and no station coordinates could be found for the address fallback.'
05009:             };
05010:         }
05011: 
05012:         stationDebug(`Coordinate fallback: ${coordinates.latitude}, ${coordinates.longitude} (${coordinates.source})`);
05013: 
05014:         const reverseResult = await fetchMissionChiefReverseAddress(coordinates.latitude, coordinates.longitude);
05015:         if (!reverseResult.address) {
05016:             return {
05017:                 address: '',
05018:                 source: coordinates.source,
05019:                 error: reverseResult.error || 'MissionChief reverse-address lookup returned no address.'
05020:             };
05021:         }
05022: 
05023:         return {
05024:             address: reverseResult.address,
05025:             source: `MissionChief reverse address via ${coordinates.source}`,
05026:             latitude: coordinates.latitude,
05027:             longitude: coordinates.longitude
05028:         };
05029:     }
05030: 
05031:     async function fetchStationMoveAddress(station) {
05032:         const url = `${station.href}/move`;
05033: 
05034:         try {
05035:             const response = await stationFetchWithTimeout(url, {
05036:                 credentials: 'same-origin',
05037:                 cache: 'no-store'
05038:             }, 9000);
05039: 
05040:             if (!response.ok) {
05041:                 return {
05042:                     address: '',
05043:                     source: 'Move building page',
05044:                     error: `Move building request returned HTTP ${response.status}.`
05045:                 };
05046:             }
05047: 
05048:             const html = await response.text();
05049:             const doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(html, 'text/html');
05050:             const address = String(doc.querySelector('#building_address')?.value || '').trim();
05051: 
05052:             if (address) {
05053:                 return {
05054:                     address,
05055:                     source: 'Move building page (background read only)'

10112:         }) || candidates[0] || null;

10115:     function getUnitClassOptionsForStationType(stationType) {
10116:         const selectedStationType = String(stationType || 'ALL');
10117:         const configuredTypeIds =
10118:             UNIT_CLASS_TYPE_IDS_BY_STATION_TYPE[selectedStationType];
10119: 
10120:         const typeIds =
10121:             Array.isArray(configuredTypeIds)
10122:                 ? configuredTypeIds
10123:                 : Object.keys(TYPE_ID_TO_VEHICLE_TYPE);
10124: 
10125:         const byVehicleType = new Map();
10126: 
10127:         typeIds.forEach(vehicleTypeId => {
10128:             const vehicleType =
10129:                 TYPE_ID_TO_VEHICLE_TYPE[String(vehicleTypeId)] || '';
10130:             const info = VEHICLE_INFO[vehicleType];
10131: 
10132:             if (!vehicleType || !info || byVehicleType.has(vehicleType)) {
10133:                 return;
10134:             }
10135: 
10136:             byVehicleType.set(vehicleType, {
10137:                 vehicleType,
10138:                 vehicleTypeId: String(vehicleTypeId),
10139:                 label:
10140:                     `${info.icon ? `${info.icon} ` : ''}${vehicleType} (${info.code})`
10141:             });
10142:         });
10143: 
10144:         return Array.from(byVehicleType.values()).sort((left, right) => {
10145:             return left.vehicleType.localeCompare(
10146:                 right.vehicleType,
10147:                 'en-GB',
10148:                 {
10149:                     numeric: true,
10150:                     sensitivity: 'base'
10151:                 }
10152:             );
10153:         });
10154:     }

10173:         const select = document.querySelector('#mc-namer-unit-class');
10174:         if (!select) return;
10175: 
10176:         const selectedStationType =
10177:             document.querySelector('#mc-namer-station-type')?.value ||
10178:             'ALL';
10179: 
10180:         const previous =
10181:             select.value ||
10182:             STATE.selectedUnitClass ||
10183:             UNIT_CLASS_ALL;
10184: 
10185:         const options =
10186:             getUnitClassOptionsForStationType(selectedStationType);
10187: 
10188:         select.innerHTML = '';
10189: 
10190:         const allOption = document.createElement('option');
10191:         allOption.value = UNIT_CLASS_ALL;
10192:         allOption.textContent = 'All classes';
10193:         select.appendChild(allOption);
10194: 
10195:         options.forEach(item => {
10196:             const option = document.createElement('option');
10197:             option.value = item.vehicleType;
10198:             option.textContent = item.label;
10199:             option.dataset.vehicleTypeId = item.vehicleTypeId;
10200:             select.appendChild(option);
10201:         });
10202: 
10203:         const available = new Set([
10204:             UNIT_CLASS_ALL,
10205:             ...options.map(item => item.vehicleType)
10206:         ]);
10207: 
10208:         select.value = available.has(previous)
10209:             ? previous
10210:             : UNIT_CLASS_ALL;
10211: 
10212:         STATE.selectedUnitClass = select.value;
10213:         select.title =
10214:             `${options.length} naming classes available for ` +
10215:             `${STATION_TYPES[selectedStationType] || selectedStationType}`;
10216:     }
10217: 
10218:     function handleUnitStationTypeChange() {
10219:         populateUnitClassDropdown();
10220:         populateStartDropdown();
10221:     }
10222: 
10223:     function handleUnitClassChange() {
10224:         STATE.selectedUnitClass = getSelectedUnitClass();
10225:         populateStartDropdown();
10226:         setStatus('Class filter updated');
10227:         log(`Unit class: ${getUnitClassDisplayLabel()}`, 'info');
10228:     }
10229: 
10230:     function vehicleMatchesSelectedUnitClass(vehicle) {
10231:         const selected =
10232:             STATE.selectedUnitClass ||
10233:             getSelectedUnitClass();
10234: 
10235:         return (
10236:             selected === UNIT_CLASS_ALL ||
10237:             vehicle?.vehicleType === selected
10238:         );
10239:     }
10240: 
10241:     function refreshStations() {
10242:         if (PERSONNEL_STATE.running) {
10243:             log('Personnel Assignment is currently running. Stop it before refreshing Unit Naming.', 'error');
10244:             return;
10245:         }
10246: 
10247:         if (STATE.running) {
10248:             log('Cannot refresh while running. Stop first.', 'error');
10249:             return;
10250:         }
10251: 
10252:         populateUnitClassDropdown();
10253:         STATE.selectedUnitClass = getSelectedUnitClass();
10254:         setStatus('Refreshing stations...');
10255:         log('Refreshing station list...', 'info');
10256:         log(`Unit class filter: ${getUnitClassDisplayLabel()}`, 'info');
10257: 
10258:         const stationEntries = getStationOverviewEntries();
10259: 
10260:         STATE.stations = stationEntries.map((entry, index) => ({
10261:             index,
10262:             href: entry.href,
10263:             displayName: entry.displayName,
10264:             callsignBase: createCallsignBase(entry.displayName),
10265:             stationType:
10266:                 STATION_BUILDING_TYPE_INFO[entry.buildingTypeId]?.stationType ||
10267:                 detectStationType(entry.displayName)
10268:         }));
10269: 
10270:         populateStartDropdown();
10271: 
10272:         setStatus('Ready');
10273:         setProgress(0, STATE.filteredStations.length);
10274: 
10275:         log(`Stations found: ${STATE.stations.length}`, 'info');
10276:         log(`Matching filter: ${STATE.filteredStations.length}`, 'info');
10277: 
10278:         if (STATE.filteredStations.length) {
10279:             log(`First match: ${STATE.filteredStations[0].displayName}`, 'info');
10280:             log(`Last match : ${STATE.filteredStations[STATE.filteredStations.length - 1].displayName}`, 'info');
10281:         }
10282:     }
10283: 
10284:     function populateStartDropdown() {
10285:         const select = document.querySelector('#mc-namer-startfrom');
10286:         if (!select) return;
10287: 
10288:         const selectedType = document.querySelector('#mc-namer-station-type')?.value || 'ALL';
10289: 
10290:         STATE.filteredStations = STATE.stations.filter(station => {
10291:             return selectedType === 'ALL' || station.stationType === selectedType;
10292:         });
10293: 
10294:         select.innerHTML = '';
10295: 
10296:         if (!STATE.filteredStations.length) {
10297:             select.innerHTML = `<option value="">No matching stations found</option>`;
10298:             setProgress(0, 0);
10299:             return;
10300:         }
10301: 
10302:         const firstOption = document.createElement('option');
10303:         firstOption.value = STATE.filteredStations[0].href;
10304:         firstOption.textContent = 'Start from first matching station';
10305:         select.appendChild(firstOption);
10306: 
10307:         STATE.filteredStations.forEach(station => {
10308:             const option = document.createElement('option');
10309:             option.value = station.href;
10310:             option.textContent = `${station.displayName} [${STATION_TYPES[station.stationType] || station.stationType}]`;
10311:             select.appendChild(option);
10312:         });
10313: 
10314:         setProgress(0, STATE.filteredStations.length);
10315:     }
10316: 
10317:     function startRun() {
10318:         if (PERSONNEL_STATE.running) {
10319:             log('Personnel Assignment is currently running. Stop it before starting Unit Naming.', 'error');
10320:             return;
10321:         }
10322: 
10323:         if (STATION_STATE.running) {
10324:             log('Station Naming is currently running. Stop it before starting Unit Naming.', 'error');
10325:             return;
10326:         }
10327: 
10328:         if (STATE.running) {
10329:             log('Already running.', 'debug');
10330:             return;
10331:         }
10332: 
10333:         if (STATE.activeIframe) {
10334:             releaseUnitIframeDocument(STATE.activeIframe);
10335:             STATE.activeIframe = null;
10336:         }
10337: 
10338:         if (!STATE.filteredStations.length) {
10339:             log('No matching stations loaded. Press Refresh Stations first.', 'error');
10340:             return;
10341:         }
10342: 
10343:         const selectedHref = document.querySelector('#mc-namer-startfrom').value;
10344:         const startIndex = STATE.filteredStations.findIndex(station => station.href === selectedHref);
10345: 
10346:         if (startIndex < 0) {
10347:             log(`Selected start station not found: ${selectedHref}`, 'error');
10348:             return;
10349:         }
10350: 
10351:         STATE.mode = document.querySelector('#mc-namer-mode')?.value || 'all';
10352:         STATE.selectedUnitClass = getSelectedUnitClass();
10353:         STATE.running = true;
10354:         STATE.paused = false;
10355:         STATE.stopped = false;
10356:         STATE.stationIndex = startIndex;
10357:         STATE.renamedCount = 0;
10358:         STATE.skippedCount = 0;
10359:         const runId = ++STATE.runId;
10360: 
10361:         updateCounters();
10362:         document.querySelector('#mc-namer-pause').textContent = 'Pause';
10363: 
10364:         log(`Mode: ${STATE.mode === 'single' ? 'Selected station only' : 'All matching stations'}`, 'info');
10365:         log(`Station type: ${STATION_TYPES[document.querySelector('#mc-namer-station-type').value]}`, 'info');
10366:         log(`Unit class: ${getUnitClassDisplayLabel(STATE.selectedUnitClass)}`, 'info');
10367:         log(`Starting from: ${STATE.filteredStations[startIndex].displayName}`, 'info');
10368: 
10369:         processStations(runId).catch(error => {
10370:             if (runId !== STATE.runId) return;
10371:             log(`Unit Naming stopped after an unexpected error: ${error?.message || error}`, 'error');
10372:             debug(error?.stack || String(error));
10373:             STATE.stopped = true;
10374:             setStatus('Error');
10375:         });
10376:     }
10377: 
10378:     function togglePause() {
10379:         if (!STATE.running) return;
10380: 
10381:         STATE.paused = !STATE.paused;
10382:         document.querySelector('#mc-namer-pause').textContent = STATE.paused ? 'Resume' : 'Pause';
10383:         setStatus(STATE.paused ? 'Paused' : 'Running');
10384:         log(STATE.paused ? 'Paused.' : 'Resumed.', 'debug');
10385:     }
10386: 
10387:     function stopRun() {
10388:         if (!STATE.running) return;
10389: 
10390:         STATE.stopped = true;
10391:         STATE.paused = false;
10392:         document.querySelector('#mc-namer-pause').textContent = 'Pause';
10393:         setStatus('Stopping...');
10394:         log('Stop requested. The current safe step will finish, then the run will close.', 'error');
10395:     }
10396: 
10397:     function toggleDebug() {
10398:         STATE.debug = !STATE.debug;
10399: 
10400:         document.querySelector('#mc-namer-debug').textContent = STATE.debug ? 'Debug: ON' : 'Debug: OFF';
10401:         log(`Debug ${STATE.debug ? 'enabled' : 'disabled'}.`, 'debug');
10402:     }
10403: 
10404:     function clearLog() {
10405:         clearBoundedToolLog(getToolElementById('mc-namer-log'));
10406:     }
10407: 
10408:     async function processStations(runId) {
10409:         try {
10410:             while (STATE.stationIndex < STATE.filteredStations.length) {
10411:                 if (STATE.stopped || runId !== STATE.runId) break;
10412: 
10413:                 await waitIfPaused(runId);
10414:                 if (STATE.stopped || runId !== STATE.runId) break;
10415: 
10416:                 const station = STATE.filteredStations[STATE.stationIndex];
10417: 
10418:                 setStatus('Opening station');
10419:                 setStation(station.displayName);
10420:                 setVehicle('None');
10421:                 setProgress(STATE.stationIndex, STATE.filteredStations.length);
10422: 
10423:                 log(`Station: ${station.displayName}`, 'station');
10424: 
10425:                 const stationEntry = findStationOverviewEntry(station.href);
10426:                 const stationLink = stationEntry?.link || null;
10427: 
10428:                 if (!stationLink) {
10429:                     log(`Station link not found, skipped: ${station.displayName}`, 'error');
10430:                     STATE.stationIndex++;
10431:                     continue;
10432:                 }
10433: 
10434:                 const iframe = await openStationWorkflowIframe(
10435:                     stationEntry,
10436:                     'unit-naming',
10437:                     waitForStationIframe
10438:                 );
10439: 
10440:                 if (iframe) {
10441:                     STATE.activeIframe = iframe;
10442:                 }
10443: 
10444:                 if (!iframe) {
10445:                     log(`Station iframe not found, skipped: ${station.displayName}`, 'error');
10446:                     STATE.skippedCount++;
10447:                     updateCounters();
10448:                     await closeStationModal(iframe);
10449:                     STATE.stationIndex++;
10450:                     continue;
10451:                 }
10452: 
10453:                 const vehicleTableReady = await waitForVehicleTable(iframe);
10454: 
10455:                 if (!vehicleTableReady) {
10456:                     log(`Vehicle table did not load, skipped: ${station.displayName}`, 'error');
10457:                     STATE.skippedCount++;
10458:                     updateCounters();
10459:                     await closeStationModal(iframe);
10460:                     STATE.stationIndex++;
10461:                     continue;
10462:                 }
10463: 
10464:                 await processStationVehicleQueue(iframe, station);
10465:                 await closeStationModal(iframe);
10466: 
10467:                 if (STATE.mode === 'single') {
10468:                     log('Single station mode complete.', 'done');
10469:                     break;
10470:                 }
10471: 
10472:                 STATE.stationIndex++;
10473:             }
10474:         } finally {
10475:             if (STATE.activeIframe) {
10476:                 await closeStationModal(STATE.activeIframe);
10477:             }
10478: 
10479:             if (runId !== STATE.runId) return;
10480: 
10481:             STATE.running = false;
10482:             STATE.paused = false;
10483:             document.querySelector('#mc-namer-pause').textContent = 'Pause';
10484: 
10485:             if (STATE.stopped) {
10486:                 setStatus('Stopped');
10487:                 log(`Stopped. Renamed ${STATE.renamedCount}. Skipped ${STATE.skippedCount}.`, 'error');
10488:             } else {
10489:                 setStatus('Finished');
10490:                 setStation('None');
10491:                 setVehicle('None');
10492:                 setProgress(
10493:                     STATE.mode === 'single' ? 1 : STATE.filteredStations.length,
10494:                     STATE.mode === 'single' ? 1 : STATE.filteredStations.length
10495:                 );
10496:                 log(`Finished. Renamed ${STATE.renamedCount}. Skipped ${STATE.skippedCount}.`, 'done');
10497:             }
10498:         }
10499:     }
10500: 
10501:     async function waitForStationIframe(stationHref, preferredIframe = null, maximumAttempts = 80) {
10502:         const expectedHref = String(stationHref || '');
10503:         const expectedBuildingId = getBuildingIdFromHref(expectedHref);
10504: 
10505:         for (let i = 0; i < maximumAttempts; i++) {
10506:             if (STATE.stopped) return null;
10507: 
10508:             const candidates = [
10509:                 preferredIframe,
10510:                 ...document.querySelectorAll(
10511:                     'iframe.lightbox_iframe, iframe#lssmv4-redesign-lightbox-iframe, ' +
10512:                     '.vm--modal iframe[src*="/buildings/"], ' +
10513:                     'iframe.mc-namer-managed-station-iframe'
10514:                 )
10515:             ].filter(Boolean);
10516: 
10517:             for (const iframe of candidates.reverse()) {
10518:                 try {
10519:                     const doc = iframe.contentDocument;
10520:                     if (!doc) continue;
10521: 
10522:                     const currentPath = iframe.contentWindow?.location?.pathname || '';
10523:                     const sourcePath = iframe.getAttribute('src') || '';
10524:                     const currentBuildingId = getBuildingIdFromHref(currentPath || sourcePath);
10525: 
10526:                     const exactStationMatch = Boolean(
10527:                         expectedHref && (
10528:                             currentPath.startsWith(expectedHref) ||
10529:                             sourcePath.startsWith(expectedHref) ||
10530:                             sourcePath.includes(expectedHref)
10531:                         )
10532:                     );
10533: 
10534:                     const buildingIdMatch = Boolean(
10535:                         expectedBuildingId && currentBuildingId === expectedBuildingId
10536:                     );
10537: 
10538:                     const documentMatch = Boolean(
10539:                         expectedHref && (
10540:                             doc.querySelector(`#vehicle_table`) ||
10541:                             doc.querySelector(`a[href="${expectedHref}/edit"]`) ||
10542:                             doc.querySelector(`a[href="${expectedHref}/move"]`) ||
10543:                             doc.querySelector(`form[action="${expectedHref}"]`)
10544:                         )
10545:                     );
10546: 
10547:                     if (exactStationMatch || buildingIdMatch || documentMatch) {
10548:                         debug(`Station iframe found: ${currentPath || sourcePath || 'unknown path'}`);
10549:                         return iframe;
10550:                     }
10551:                 } catch (error) {
10552:                     debug(`Station iframe access retry: ${error?.message || error}`);
10553:                 }
10554:             }
10555: 
10556:             await sleep(250);
10557:         }
10558: 
10559:         return null;
10560:     }
10561: 
10562:     async function waitForVehicleTable(iframe) {
10563:         for (let i = 0; i < 80; i++) {
10564:             if (STATE.stopped) return false;
10565: 
10566:             try {
10567:                 const doc = iframe.contentDocument;
10568:                 if (doc && doc.querySelector('#vehicle_table tbody tr')) {
10569:                     await sleep(300);
10570:                     return true;
10571:                 }
10572:             } catch (e) {}
10573: 
10574:             await sleep(250);
10575:         }
10576:         return false;
10577:     }
10578: 
10579:     async function waitForEditPage(iframe) {
10580:         for (let i = 0; i < 80; i++) {
10581:             if (STATE.stopped) return false;
10582: 
10583:             try {
10584:                 const doc = iframe.contentDocument;
10585:                 if (doc && doc.querySelector('#vehicle_caption')) {
10586:                     await sleep(300);
10587:                     return true;
10588:                 }
10589:             } catch (e) {}
10590: 
10591:             await sleep(250);
10592:         }
10593:         return false;
10594:     }
10595: 
10596:     function getVehicleQueueFromTable(doc) {
10597:         return [...doc.querySelectorAll('#vehicle_table tbody tr')]
10598:             .map((row, index) => {
10599:                 const img = row.querySelector('img[vehicle_type_id]');
10600:                 const vehicleTypeId = img?.getAttribute('vehicle_type_id') || '';
10601:                 const vehicleType = TYPE_ID_TO_VEHICLE_TYPE[vehicleTypeId] || '';
10602: 
10603:                 const allLinks = [...row.querySelectorAll('a[href^="/vehicles/"]')];
10604: 
10605:                 const editLink = allLinks.find(a => {
10606:                     const href = a.getAttribute('href') || '';
10607:                     return href.endsWith('/edit');
10608:                 });
10609: 
10610:                 const vehicleLink = allLinks.find(a => {
10611:                     const href = a.getAttribute('href') || '';
10612:                     return !href.endsWith('/edit') && !href.includes('/zuweisung');
10613:                 });
10614: 
10615:                 const vehicleHref = vehicleLink?.getAttribute('href') || '';
10616:                 const editHref = editLink?.getAttribute('href') || '';
10617:                 const vehicleId = getVehicleIdFromHref(editHref || vehicleHref);
10618:                 const currentName = cleanText(vehicleLink?.textContent || '');
10619: 
10620:                 return {
10621:                     tableIndex: index,
10622:                     vehicleId,
10623:                     vehicleTypeId,
10624:                     vehicleType,
10625:                     currentName,
10626:                     vehicleHref,
10627:                     editHref
10628:                 };
10629:             })
10630:             .filter(v => v.editHref && v.vehicleId)
10631:             .filter(vehicleMatchesSelectedUnitClass);
10632:     }
10633: 
10634:     async function processStationVehicleQueue(iframe, station) {
10635:         setStatus('Building vehicle queue');
10636: 
10637:         // Build a lightweight string-only queue and release the station document
10638:         // before the first navigation. Holding the original document across every
10639:         // awaited edit page kept the full station DOM alive for the whole run.
10640:         const queue = getVehicleQueueFromTable(
10641:             iframe.contentDocument
10642:         );
10643: 
10644:         debug(`Vehicle queue built for ${station.displayName}: ${queue.length}`);
10645: 
10646:         if (!queue.length) {
10647:             log(`No ${getUnitClassDisplayLabel(STATE.selectedUnitClass)} vehicles found in ${station.displayName}.`, 'debug');
10648:             return;
10649:         }
10650: 
10651:         const counts = {};
10652: 
10653:         for (const item of queue) {
10654:             if (STATE.stopped) return;
10655: 
10656:             await waitIfPaused();
10657:             if (STATE.stopped) return;
10658: 
10659:             if (!item.vehicleType) {
10660:                 log(`Unknown vehicle_type_id skipped: ${item.vehicleTypeId} | ${item.editHref}`, 'error');
10661:                 STATE.skippedCount++;
10662:                 updateCounters();
10663:                 continue;
10664:             }
10665: 
10666:             const info = VEHICLE_INFO[item.vehicleType];
10667: 
10668:             if (!info) {
10669:                 log(`No naming rule skipped: "${item.vehicleType}"`, 'error');
10670:                 STATE.skippedCount++;
10671:                 updateCounters();
10672:                 continue;
10673:             }
10674: 
10675:             counts[item.vehicleType] = (counts[item.vehicleType] || 0) + 1;
10676: 
10677:             const newName = makeVehicleName(
10678:                 station,
10679:                 item.vehicleType,
10680:                 counts[item.vehicleType]
10681:             );
10682: 
10683:             setStatus('Opening edit page');
10684:             setVehicle(item.editHref);
10685: 
10686:             // Replace rather than append to the iframe history. This prevents a
10687:             // long rename run from retaining one browsing-history document for
10688:             // every vehicle edit page.
10689:             if (!navigateUnitIframe(iframe, item.editHref)) {
10690:                 log(`Could not navigate to vehicle edit page: ${item.editHref}`, 'error');
10691:                 STATE.skippedCount++;
10692:                 updateCounters();
10693:                 continue;
10694:             }
10695: 
10696:             const editPageReady = await waitForEditPage(iframe);
10697: 
10698:             if (!editPageReady) {
10699:                 log(`Vehicle edit page did not load: ${item.editHref}`, 'error');
10700:                 STATE.skippedCount++;
10701:                 updateCounters();
10702:                 continue;
10703:             }
10704: 
10705:             let editDoc = iframe.contentDocument;
10706:             let captionInput = editDoc?.querySelector('#vehicle_caption') || null;
10707:             let saveBtn = editDoc?.querySelector(
10708:                 'input[type="submit"][value="Save"], button[type="submit"]'
10709:             ) || null;
10710: 
10711:             if (!captionInput || !saveBtn) {
10712:                 log(`Caption or Save missing: ${item.editHref}`, 'error');
10713:                 STATE.skippedCount++;
10714:                 updateCounters();
10715:                 editDoc = null;
10716:                 captionInput = null;
10717:                 saveBtn = null;
10718:                 continue;
10719:             }
10720: 
10721:             const before = captionInput.value;
10722: 
10723:             setStatus('Saving vehicle');
10724:             setVehicle(newName);
10725: 
10726:             captionInput.value = newName;
10727:             captionInput.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
10728:             captionInput.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
10729: 
10730:             log(`BEFORE: ${before}`, 'before');
10731:             log(`AFTER : ${newName}`, 'after');
10732: 
10733:             saveBtn.click();
10734: 
10735:             STATE.renamedCount++;
10736:             updateCounters();
10737: 
10738:             // Do not retain the completed edit document or its form controls
10739:             // during the post-save wait.
10740:             editDoc = null;
10741:             captionInput = null;
10742:             saveBtn = null;
10743: 
10744:             await sleep(700);
10745:         }
10746: 
10747:         navigateUnitIframe(iframe, station.href);
10748:         await waitForVehicleTable(iframe);
10749:         await sleep(300);
10750:     }
10751: 
10752:     async function closeStationModal(iframe = STATE.activeIframe) {
10753:         setStatus('Closing station');
10754: 
10755:         const activeIframe = iframe || STATE.activeIframe;
10756:         if (STATE.activeIframe === activeIframe) {
10757:             STATE.activeIframe = null;
10758:         }
10759: 
10760:         if (removeManagedStationIframe(activeIframe)) {
10761:             await sleep(50);
10762:             return;
10763:         }
10764: 
10765:         const closeBtn = getUnitModalCloseButton(activeIframe);
10766: 
10767:         if (closeBtn) {
10768:             closeBtn.click();
10769: 
10770:             // Give the framework a short opportunity to detach or hide the
10771:             // correct modal before its iframe document is cleared.
10772:             for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 15; attempt++) {
10773:                 const modal = activeIframe?.closest?.(
10774:                     '.vm--modal, [role="dialog"], .lightbox, .modal'
10775:                 );
10776: 
10777:                 if (
10778:                     !activeIframe?.isConnected ||
10779:                     (modal && modal.offsetParent === null)
10780:                 ) {
10781:                     break;
10782:                 }
10783: 
10784:                 await sleep(100);
10785:             }
10786:         } else {
10787:             log('Close button not found.', 'error');
10788:         }
10789: 
10790:         // MissionChief may hide and reuse the modal instead of removing it.
10791:         // Blank the associated iframe so its station/edit documents and history
10792:         // become collectible before the next station is opened.
10793:         releaseUnitIframeDocument(activeIframe);
10794:         await sleep(150);
10795:     }
10796: 
10797:     async function waitIfPaused(runId = STATE.runId) {
10798:         while (STATE.paused && !STATE.stopped && runId === STATE.runId) {
10799:             await sleep(300);
10800:         }
10801:     }
10802: 
10803:     function setStatus(text) {
10804:         const el = getToolElementById('mc-namer-status');
10805:         if (el) el.textContent = text;
10806:     }
10807: 
10808:     function setStation(text) {
10809:         const el = getToolElementById('mc-namer-station');
10810:         if (el) el.textContent = text;

09031:     function parsePersonnelVehicleOccupancy(row) {
09032:         const text = String(row?.textContent || '');
09033:         const matches = [...text.matchAll(/\(\s*(\d+)\s*\/\s*(\d+)\s*\)/g)];
09034:         const match = matches.at(-1);
09035: 
09036:         if (!match) {
09037:             return { assignedCount: null, maxCount: null };
09038:         }
09039: 
09040:         return {
09041:             assignedCount: Number(match[1]),
09042:             maxCount: Number(match[2])
09043:         };
09044:     }

09053:     function getPersonnelAmbulanceQueue(doc) {
09054:         const seen = new Set();
09055:         const queue = [];
09056:         const rows = [...doc.querySelectorAll('#vehicle_table tr')];
09057:         let ambulanceRows = 0;
09058: 
09059:         for (const row of rows) {
09060:             const image = row.querySelector('img[vehicle_type_id]');
09061:             const vehicleTypeId = image?.getAttribute('vehicle_type_id') || '';
09062:             if (vehicleTypeId !== PERSONNEL_TARGET_VEHICLE_TYPE_ID) continue;
09063: 
09064:             ambulanceRows++;
09065: 
09066:             const vehicleLinks = [...row.querySelectorAll('a[href*="/vehicles/"]')];
09067:             const plainVehicleLink = vehicleLinks.find(link => {
09068:                 const href = link.getAttribute('href') || '';
09069:                 return /^\/vehicles\/\d+\/?(?:[?#].*)?$/.test(href);
09070:             });
09071:             const editLink = vehicleLinks.find(link => {
09072:                 const href = link.getAttribute('href') || '';
09073:                 return /\/vehicles\/\d+\/edit(?:[/?#]|$)/.test(href);
09074:             });
09075:             const directAssignmentLink = vehicleLinks.find(link => {
09076:                 const href = link.getAttribute('href') || '';
09077:                 return /\/vehicles\/\d+\/zuweisung(?:[/?#]|$)/.test(href);
09078:             });
09079: 
09080:             // Prefer the normal vehicle-name link because it is tied directly to the
09081:             // displayed row. Edit and personnel links are safe fallbacks.
09082:             const idCandidates = [plainVehicleLink, editLink, directAssignmentLink]
09083:                 .map(link => getVehicleIdFromHref(link?.getAttribute('href') || ''))
09084:                 .filter(Boolean);
09085:             const vehicleId = idCandidates[0] || '';
09086: 
09087:             if (!vehicleId || seen.has(vehicleId)) continue;
09088:             seen.add(vehicleId);
09089: 
09090:             const distinctIds = [...new Set(idCandidates)];
09091:             if (distinctIds.length > 1) {
09092:                 personnelDebug(`Ambulance row contains conflicting vehicle IDs (${distinctIds.join(', ')}). Using ${vehicleId}.`);
09093:             }
09094: 
09095:             const assignmentHref = directAssignmentLink?.getAttribute('href') || `/vehicles/${vehicleId}/zuweisung`;
09096:             const nameSource = plainVehicleLink
09097:                 || vehicleLinks.find(link => cleanText(link.textContent || ''))
09098:                 || null;
09099:             const name = cleanText(nameSource?.textContent || row.textContent || '') || `Ambulance ${vehicleId}`;
09100:             const occupancy = parsePersonnelVehicleOccupancy(row);
09101: 
09102:             queue.push({
09103:                 vehicleId,
09104:                 name,
09105:                 assignmentHref,
09106:                 assignedCount: occupancy.assignedCount,
09107:                 maxCount: occupancy.maxCount
09108:             });
09109:         }
09110: 
09111:         queue.sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name, undefined, {
09112:             numeric: true,
09113:             sensitivity: 'base'
09114:         }));
09115: 
09116:         personnelDebug(`Vehicle table scan: ${rows.length} row(s), ${ambulanceRows} Ambulance row(s), ${queue.length} usable assignment route(s).`);
09117:         queue.forEach(vehicle => {
09118:             personnelDebug(`Ambulance detected: ${vehicle.name} | ID=${vehicle.vehicleId} | table occupancy=${vehicle.assignedCount ?? '?'} / ${vehicle.maxCount ?? '?'}`);
09119:         });
09120: 
09121:         return queue;
09122:     }

09125:     function parseTrainingCodes(row) {
09126:         const raw = row?.getAttribute('data-filterable-by') ||
09127:             row?.querySelector?.('[data-filterable-by]')?.getAttribute('data-filterable-by') ||
09128:             '[]';
09129:         try {
09130:             const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
09131:             return Array.isArray(parsed)
09132:                 ? Array.from(new Set(parsed.map(String).filter(Boolean)))
09133:                 : [];
09134:         } catch (_error) {
09135:             const rawText = String(raw || '');
09136:             const quotedCodes = Array.from(
09137:                 rawText.matchAll(/["']([^"']+)["']/g),
09138:                 match => String(match[1] || '').trim()
09139:             ).filter(Boolean);
09140: 
09141:             if (quotedCodes.length) {
09142:                 return Array.from(new Set(quotedCodes));
09143:             }
09144: 
09145:             return Array.from(new Set(
09146:                 rawText
09147:                     .replace(/[\[\]]/g, ' ')
09148:                     .split(/[\s,]+/)
09149:                     .map(value => value.replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '').trim())
09150:                     .filter(Boolean)
09151:             ));
09152:         }
09153:     }

09163:             row.querySelector?.('input.personal-delete-checkbox[value]')?.getAttribute?.('value'),
09164:             row.querySelector?.('a[href^="/personals/"]')?.getAttribute?.('href')?.match(/\/personals\/(\d+)/)?.[1]
09165:         ];
09166: 
09167:         return String(
09168:             candidates.find(value => /^\d+$/.test(String(value || '').trim())) ||
09169:             ''
09170:         );

09173:     function getUniquePersonnelVehicleNameIndex(vehicles) {
09174:         const index = new Map();
09175: 
09176:         (Array.isArray(vehicles) ? vehicles : []).forEach(vehicle => {
09177:             const key = normalizePersonnelVehicleName(vehicle?.name || '');
09178:             if (!key || !vehicle?.vehicleId) return;
09179: 
09180:             if (index.has(key)) {
09181:                 index.set(key, null);
09182:                 return;
09183:             }
09184: 
09185:             index.set(key, vehicle);
09186:         });
09187: 
09188:         return index;
09189:     }

09390:     async function submitPersonnelAssignment(candidate) {
09391:         if (!candidate.assignHref || PERSONNEL_STATE.stopped) return false;
09392: 
09393:         const csrfToken = document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]')?.getAttribute('content') || '';
09394:         const headers = {
09395:             'Accept': 'text/html,application/xhtml+xml',
09396:             'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest'
09397:         };
09398:         if (csrfToken) headers['X-CSRF-Token'] = csrfToken;
09399: 
09400:         const response = await personnelFetchResponse(candidate.assignHref, {
09401:             method: 'POST',
09402:             headers
09403:         }, 12000);
09404: 
09405:         if (!response.ok) {
09406:             personnelDebug(`Assignment request HTTP ${response.status}: ${candidate.assignHref}`);
09407:             return false;
09408:         }
09409: 
09410:         // Consume the response so the request finishes cleanly. MissionChief normally
09411:         // returns the updated personnel-row HTML, but the batch is verified using one
09412:         // fresh vehicle page after all required people have been submitted.
09413:         try {
09414:             await response.text();
09415:         } catch (_error) {}
09416: 
09417:         return true;
09418:     }

09424:             return report?.stationTargetMet

09430:         if (Number(report?.assignmentShortfall || 0) > 0) return 'SHORTFALL';
09431:         return 'COMPLETE';

09450:     function buildStationAfterActionText(report) {
09451:         if (!report) return 'No station report is available yet.';
09452: 
09453:         const isAssign = PERSONNEL_STATE.action === 'assign';
09454:         const service = getPersonnelServiceDefinition();

09457:         const qualificationLabel = getPersonnelReportQualificationLabel(report);

09459:             `${service.label.toUpperCase()} — ${profile.label.toUpperCase()} ${isAssign ? 'STATION AFTER-ACTION REPORT' : 'STATION PREVIEW REPORT'}`,

09463:             ''
09464:         ];
09465:

09472:             lines.push('No vehicles mapped to this profile were found at the station. No assignments were changed.');
09473:             return lines.join('\n');
09474:         }
09475:

09477:             ? (report.afterActionVerified ? 'PASSED - final station state re-read' : `PARTIAL - ${report.verificationError || 'final station re-read was not verified'}`)
09478:             : 'PREVIEW PROJECTION - no assignments changed';
09479:

09485:                     ? [

09489:                     ]
09490:                     : []

09494:             `${qualificationLabel} personnel visible: ${Number(report.trained || 0)}`,
09495:             `Available qualified before action: ${Number(report.availableAtScan || 0)}`,
09496:             `Available qualified after action: ${Number(report.availableAtEnd || 0)}`,
09497:             `Qualified positions before: ${getPersonnelReportInitialFilled(report)}/${Number(report.required || 0)}`,
09498:             `${isAssign ? 'Qualified positions after' : 'Projected qualified positions'}: ${getPersonnelReportFinalFilled(report)}/${Number(report.required || 0)}`,
09499:             `${isAssign ? 'Personnel assigned and verified' : 'Assignments planned'}: ${isAssign ? Number(report.assigned || 0) : Number(report.planned || 0)}`,
09500:             `Qualification shortfall estimate: ${Number(report.trainingShortfall || 0)}`,
09501:             `Missing training by qualification: ${formatPoliceTrainingGapSummary(report.missingTrainingSummary)}`,
09502:             `Unfilled required positions after action: ${Number(report.assignmentShortfall || 0)}`
09503:         );
09504: 
09505:         if (Array.isArray(report.rules) && report.rules.length > 1) {
09506:             lines.push('', 'RULE GROUPS');
09507:             report.rules.forEach(rule => {
09508:                 lines.push(
09509:                     `  - ${rule.label}: ${rule.vehicleCount} ${rule.vehicleLabel}(s) | target ${rule.target} each | required ${rule.required} | trained ${rule.trained} | training shortfall ${rule.trainingShortfall} | missing ${formatPoliceTrainingGapSummary(rule.trainingGaps)}`
09510:                 );
09511:             });
09512:         }
09513: 
09514:         lines.push('', 'VEHICLE RESULTS');
09515: 
09516:         report.vehicles.forEach(vehicle => {
09517:             const names = isAssign ? vehicle.assignedNames : vehicle.plannedNames;
09518:             const afterLabel = isAssign
09519:                 ? (vehicle.verificationStatus === 'final-verified' ? 'final verified' : 'after action')
09520:                 : 'projected';
09521:             const target = Number(vehicle.target || PERSONNEL_TARGET_PER_VEHICLE);

09711:     function getPersonnelTrainingShortfallReports() {
09712:         if (PERSONNEL_SHORTFALL_CACHE_SIZE === PERSONNEL_STATE.reports.length) {
09713:             return PERSONNEL_SHORTFALL_CACHE;
09714:         }
09715: 
09716:         PERSONNEL_SHORTFALL_CACHE = PERSONNEL_STATE.reports
09717:             .filter(report => !report?.error && Number(report?.trainingShortfall || 0) > 0)
09718:             .slice()
09719:             .sort((left, right) => {
09720:                 const shortfallDifference = Number(right.trainingShortfall || 0) - Number(left.trainingShortfall || 0);
09721:                 if (shortfallDifference) return shortfallDifference;
09722:                 return String(left.stationName || '').localeCompare(String(right.stationName || ''), 'en-GB', { numeric: true });
09723:             });
09724:         PERSONNEL_SHORTFALL_CACHE_SIZE = PERSONNEL_STATE.reports.length;
09725:         return PERSONNEL_SHORTFALL_CACHE;
09726:     }
