07864:     function selectPoliceRuleVehicles({ rule, allVehicles, personnel, reservedPersonnelIds, claimedVehicleIds, batch }) {
07865:         const matchingVehicles = allVehicles.filter(vehicle => rule.vehicleTypeIds.includes(vehicle.vehicleTypeId) && !claimedVehicleIds.has(vehicle.vehicleId));
07866:         if (!batch || !rule.sharedVehiclePool) return matchingVehicles;
07867: 
07868:         const existing = matchingVehicles.filter(vehicle =>
07869:             getPersonnelAssignedToVehicle(personnel, vehicle).some(person => personnelMatchesRule(person, rule))
07870:         );
07871:         const existingIds = new Set(existing.map(vehicle => vehicle.vehicleId));
07872:         const availableCount = personnel.filter(person =>
07873:             person.available
07874:             && !reservedPersonnelIds.has(person.personnelId)
07875:             && personnelMatchesRule(person, rule)
07876:         ).length;
07877:         const additionalVehicleCount = Math.ceil(availableCount / Math.max(1, rule.target));
07878:         const additional = matchingVehicles
07879:             .filter(vehicle => !existingIds.has(vehicle.vehicleId))
07880:             .sort((left, right) => {
07881:                 const leftOccupied = Number.isInteger(left.assignedCount) ? left.assignedCount : 0;
07882:                 const rightOccupied = Number.isInteger(right.assignedCount) ? right.assignedCount : 0;
07883:                 if (leftOccupied !== rightOccupied) return leftOccupied - rightOccupied;
07884:                 return left.name.localeCompare(right.name, 'en-GB', { numeric: true });
07885:             })
07886:             .slice(0, additionalVehicleCount);
07887: 
07888:         return [...existing, ...additional];
07889:     }
07890: 
07891:     function calculatePersonnelQualificationShortfall(personnel, slotRules) {
07892:         const people = (personnel || []).filter(person => person?.personnelId);
07893:         const slots = Array.isArray(slotRules) ? slotRules : [];
07894:         const personToSlot = new Map();
07895:         const candidatesByRule = new Map();
07896: 
07897:         const getCandidates = rule => {
07898:             if (!candidatesByRule.has(rule)) {
07899:                 candidatesByRule.set(
07900:                     rule,
07901:                     people.filter(person => personnelMatchesRule(person, rule))
07902:                 );
07903:             }
07904:             return candidatesByRule.get(rule);
07905:         };
07906: 
07907:         const tryFillSlot = (slotIndex, seenPersonnel) => {
07908:             const rule = slots[slotIndex];
07909:             for (const person of getCandidates(rule)) {
07910:                 if (seenPersonnel.has(person.personnelId)) continue;
07911:                 seenPersonnel.add(person.personnelId);
07912: 
07913:                 const previousSlot = personToSlot.get(person.personnelId);
07914:                 if (previousSlot === undefined || tryFillSlot(previousSlot, seenPersonnel)) {
07915:                     personToSlot.set(person.personnelId, slotIndex);
07916:                     return true;
07917:                 }
07918:             }
07919:             return false;
07920:         };
07921: 
07922:         let matched = 0;
07923:         for (let slotIndex = 0; slotIndex < slots.length; slotIndex++) {
07924:             if (tryFillSlot(slotIndex, new Set())) matched++;
07925:         }
07926: 
07927:         return Math.max(0, slots.length - matched);
07928:     }

07930:     async function processOnePoliceStation(station, profile) {
07931:         const stationPage = await personnelFetchDocument(station.href, 14000);
07932:         const rules = Array.isArray(profile.rules) ? profile.rules : [];
07933:         const targetTypeIds = [...new Set(rules.flatMap(rule => rule.vehicleTypeIds))];

07935:

07937:             personnelLog('No vehicles mapped to the selected Police profile were found at this station.', 'debug');

08001:         if (Number.isFinite(stationUnitsNeeded)) {

08003:         }

08035:                 ruleId: rule.id,

08488:     function sortMedicalVehiclesForStationTarget(
08489:         vehicles,
08490:         personnel
08491:     ) {
08492:         return (
08493:             Array.isArray(vehicles)
08494:                 ? vehicles.slice()
08495:                 : []
08496:         ).sort((left, right) => {
08497:             const getState = vehicle => {
08498:                 const assigned =
08499:                     getPersonnelAssignedToVehicle(
08500:                         personnel,
08501:                         vehicle
08502:                     );
08503: 
08504:                 const critical =
08505:                     assigned.filter(
08506:                         person =>
08507:                             person.trainingCodes.includes(
08508:                                 PERSONNEL_TRAINING_CODE
08509:                             )
08510:                     ).length;
08511: 
08512:                 const total = assigned.length;
08513: 
08514:                 return {
08515:                     covered:
08516:                         critical >=
08517:                         PERSONNEL_TARGET_PER_VEHICLE,
08518:                     critical,
08519:                     total,
08520:                     seatsAvailable:
08521:                         Math.max(
08522:                             0,
08523:                             PERSONNEL_TARGET_PER_VEHICLE
08524:                             - total
08525:                         )
08526:                 };
08527:             };
08528: 
08529:             const leftState = getState(left);
08530:             const rightState = getState(right);
08531: 
08532:             const leftRank =
08533:                 leftState.covered
08534:                     ? 0
08535:                     : leftState.seatsAvailable > 0
08536:                         ? 1
08537:                         : 2;
08538: 
08539:             const rightRank =
08540:                 rightState.covered
08541:                     ? 0
08542:                     : rightState.seatsAvailable > 0
08543:                         ? 1

===== SCORE 502 LINES 8558-8992 =====
08558:             }
08559: 
08560:             return String(
08561:                 left.name || ''
08562:             ).localeCompare(
08563:                 String(right.name || ''),
08564:                 'en-GB',
08565:                 { numeric: true }
08566:             );
08567:         });
08568:     }

08570:     async function processOneMedicalCriticalCareStation(station) {
08571:         const stationPage = await personnelFetchDocument(station.href, 14000);
08572:         const vehicles = getPersonnelAmbulanceQueue(stationPage.doc);
08573: 
08574:         if (!vehicles.length) {
08575:             throw new Error('No Ambulance vehicles were found in the station vehicle table.');
08576:         }
08577: 
08578:         // One Ambulance assignment page supplies the station-wide personnel snapshot.
08579:         // Button state is the authoritative binding signal; the station vehicle table
08580:         // supplies the initial occupied/max cross-check.
08581:         const baselinePage = await personnelFetchDocument(vehicles[0].assignmentHref, 14000);
08582:         const baselineAssignment = parseVehicleAssignmentPage(baselinePage.doc, vehicles[0].vehicleId);
08583: 
08584:         if (baselineAssignment.vehicleTypeId !== PERSONNEL_TARGET_VEHICLE_TYPE_ID) {
08585:             throw new Error(`The personnel assignment page was not an Ambulance page (vehicle_type_id=${baselineAssignment.vehicleTypeId || 'unknown'}).`);
08586:         }
08587: 
08588:         const personnel = baselineAssignment.rows;
08589:         const trained = personnel.filter(person => person.trainingCodes.includes(PERSONNEL_TRAINING_CODE));
08590:         const availableAtScan = trained.filter(person => person.available).length;
08591: 
08592:         const processingVehicles =
08593:             isPersonnelPerStationTargetMode()
08594:                 ? sortMedicalVehiclesForStationTarget(
08595:                     vehicles,
08596:                     personnel
08597:                 )
08598:                 : vehicles;
08599: 
08600:         const reservedPersonnelIds = new Set();
08601:         const vehicleReports = [];
08602:         let required = 0;
08603:         let trainingShortfall = 0;
08604:         let stationPlanned = 0;
08605:         let stationAssigned = 0;
08606:         let stationAssignmentShortfall = 0;
08607:         let projectedFulfilledUnits = 0;
08608: 
08609:         const stationUnitsNeeded = getPersonnelUnitsRemaining();
08610:         const stationUnitTargetReached = () =>
08611:             Number.isFinite(stationUnitsNeeded)
08612:             && projectedFulfilledUnits >= stationUnitsNeeded;
08613: 
08614:         personnelLog(`Ambulances available: ${vehicles.length}`, 'info');
08615:         personnelLog(`${PERSONNEL_TRAINING_LABEL} trained: ${trained.length} | Available at scan: ${availableAtScan}`, 'info');
08616:         if (Number.isFinite(stationUnitsNeeded)) {
08617:             personnelLog(`Units still required before this station: ${stationUnitsNeeded}. Only Ambulances reaching 2/2 Critical Care count.`, 'info');
08618:         }
08619:         personnelLog(
08620:             PERSONNEL_STATE.action === 'preview'
08621:                 ? 'Low-impact preview: station occupancy is cross-checked against one Ambulance personnel page.'
08622:                 : 'Fast safe assignment: each Ambulance is read once, assignments are submitted in a small batch, then verified with one vehicle check and one final station-wide check.',
08623:             'debug'
08624:         );
08625: 
08626:         for (let vehicleIndex = 0; vehicleIndex < processingVehicles.length; vehicleIndex++) {
08627:             if (PERSONNEL_STATE.stopped || stationUnitTargetReached()) break;
08628:             await waitIfPersonnelPaused();
08629:             if (PERSONNEL_STATE.stopped || stationUnitTargetReached()) break;
08630: 
08631:             const vehicle = processingVehicles[vehicleIndex];
08632:             required += PERSONNEL_TARGET_PER_VEHICLE;
08633: 
08634:             setPersonnelUiValue('vehicle', vehicle.name || vehicle.vehicleId);
08635:             setPersonnelUiValue('status', PERSONNEL_STATE.action === 'assign' ? 'Checking vehicle' : 'Building station preview');
08636:             personnelLog(`Vehicle ${vehicleIndex + 1}/${processingVehicles.length}: ${vehicle.name || vehicle.vehicleId}`, 'station');
08637: 
08638:             const stationAssignedPeople = getPersonnelAssignedToVehicle(personnel, vehicle);
08639:             const mappedTotal = stationAssignedPeople.length;
08640:             const mappedCritical = stationAssignedPeople.filter(person => person.trainingCodes.includes(PERSONNEL_TRAINING_CODE)).length;
08641:             const hasTableOccupancy = Number.isInteger(vehicle.assignedCount) && vehicle.assignedCount >= 0;
08642: 
08643:             let beforeTotal = hasTableOccupancy
08644:                 ? Math.min(vehicle.assignedCount, mappedTotal)
08645:                 : mappedTotal;
08646:             let beforeCritical = Math.min(mappedCritical, beforeTotal);
08647:             const plannedNames = [];
08648:             const assignedNames = [];
08649: 
08650:             if (hasTableOccupancy && mappedTotal !== beforeTotal) {
08651:                 personnelDebug(
08652:                     `${vehicle.name}: station table occupancy=${vehicle.assignedCount}, `
08653:                     + `personnel snapshot mapped=${mappedTotal}. Initial count set to ${beforeTotal}.`
08654:                 );
08655:             }
08656: 
08657:             if (beforeTotal === 0) beforeCritical = 0;
08658: 
08659:             // In live mode, read the specific Ambulance immediately before changing it.
08660:             let liveAssignment = null;
08661:             if (PERSONNEL_STATE.action === 'assign' && beforeCritical < PERSONNEL_TARGET_PER_VEHICLE && beforeTotal < PERSONNEL_TARGET_PER_VEHICLE) {
08662:                 const livePage = await personnelFetchDocument(vehicle.assignmentHref, 14000);
08663:                 liveAssignment = parseVehicleAssignmentPage(livePage.doc, vehicle.vehicleId);
08664: 
08665:                 if (liveAssignment.vehicleTypeId !== PERSONNEL_TARGET_VEHICLE_TYPE_ID) {

08977:     async function waitPersonnelDelay(delayMs) {
08978:         const endAt = Date.now() + Math.max(0, Number(delayMs) || 0);
08979:         while (Date.now() < endAt) {
08980:             if (PERSONNEL_STATE.stopped) return;
08981:             await sleep(Math.min(100, endAt - Date.now()));
08982:         }
08983:     }
08984: 
08985:     async function waitForPersonnelRequestSlot() {
08986:         const elapsed = Date.now() - (PERSONNEL_STATE.lastRequestAt || 0);
08987:         const waitMs = Math.max(0, PERSONNEL_REQUEST_GAP_MS - elapsed);
08988:         if (waitMs) await waitPersonnelDelay(waitMs);
08989:         if (PERSONNEL_STATE.stopped) throw new DOMException('Personnel run stopped', 'AbortError');
08990:     }

02146:         const compactDisclosureStoragePrefix =
02147:             'mc_compact_disclosure_v1071_';
02148: 
02149:         function createCompactDisclosure(
02150:             id,
02151:             label,
02152:             targets,
02153:             defaultOpen = false
02154:         ) {
02155:             const nodes = (Array.isArray(targets) ? targets : [targets])
02156:                 .filter(node => node?.isConnected);
02157:             if (!nodes.length) return null;
02158: 
02159:             const details = document.createElement('details');
02160:             details.id = id;
02161:             details.className = 'mc-compact-disclosure';
02162: 
02163:             const storageKey =
02164:                 `${compactDisclosureStoragePrefix}${id}`;
02165:             let savedState = null;
02166:             try {
02167:                 savedState = localStorage.getItem(storageKey);
02168:             } catch (_error) {}
02169:             details.open = savedState == null
02170:                 ? Boolean(defaultOpen)
02171:                 : savedState === 'true';
02172: 
02173:             const summary = document.createElement('summary');
02174:             summary.className = 'mc-compact-disclosure-summary';
02175:             summary.innerHTML = `
02176:                 <span>${label}</span>

===== SCORE 480 LINES 190-1112 =====
00190:             gw_gefahrgut:
00191:                 'HazMat Unit',
00192:             railway_fire:
00193:                 'Railway Fire'
00194:         });
00195:

01035:     function runToolLifecycleCleanup() {
01036:         if (TOOL_LIFECYCLE_CLEANED) return;
01037:         TOOL_LIFECYCLE_CLEANED = true;
01038: 
01039:         STATE.stopped = true;
01040:         STATE.paused = false;
01041: 
01042:         const activeUnitIframe = STATE.activeIframe;
01043:         STATE.activeIframe = null;
01044:         releaseUnitIframeDocument(activeUnitIframe);
01045:         STATE.stations = [];
01046:         STATE.filteredStations = [];
01047:         STATION_STATE.stopped = true;
01048:         STATION_STATE.paused = false;
01049:         PERSONNEL_STATE.stopped = true;
01050:         PERSONNEL_STATE.paused = false;
01051:

01053: 
01054:         if (PERSONNEL_STATE.activeController) {
01055:             try {
01056:                 PERSONNEL_STATE.activeController.abort();
01057:             } catch (_error) {}
01058:             PERSONNEL_STATE.activeController = null;
01059:         }
01060: 
01061:         for (const controller of STATION_STATE.activeControllers) {
01062:             try {
01063:                 controller.abort();
01064:             } catch (_error) {}
01065:         }
01066:         STATION_STATE.activeControllers.clear();
01067: 
01068:         for (const cleanup of [...TOOL_LIFECYCLE_CLEANUPS]) {
01069:             try {
01070:                 cleanup();
01071:             } catch (_error) {}
01072:         }
01073:         TOOL_LIFECYCLE_CLEANUPS.clear();
01074: 
01075:         for (const frameId of TOOL_LOG_SCROLL_FRAMES.values()) {
01076:             try {
01077:                 cancelAnimationFrame(frameId);
01078:             } catch (_error) {}
01079:         }
01080:         TOOL_LOG_SCROLL_FRAMES.clear();
01081:         TOOL_UI_ELEMENT_CACHE.clear();
01082:         PERSONNEL_STATION_LINK_BY_HREF.clear();
01083:         PERSONNEL_START_OPTION_BY_HREF.clear();
01084:         PERSONNEL_HIGHLIGHTED_STATION_LINKS.clear();

01088:         STATION_STATE.buildingDataPromise = null;
01089:         PERSONNEL_STATE.reports = [];
01090:         PERSONNEL_STATE.lastStationReport = null;

01098:     function getToolElementById(id) {
01099:         const key = String(id || '');
01100:         if (!key) return null;
01101: 
01102:         const cached = TOOL_UI_ELEMENT_CACHE.get(key);
01103:         if (cached?.isConnected) return cached;
01104: 
01105:         const element = document.getElementById(key);
01106:         if (element) TOOL_UI_ELEMENT_CACHE.set(key, element);
01107:         else TOOL_UI_ELEMENT_CACHE.delete(key);
01108:         return element;
01109:     }

05306:     function normalizeCoordinates(latitude, longitude) {
05307:         const lat = Number(latitude);
05308:         const lng = Number(longitude);
05309: 
05310:         if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || !Number.isFinite(lng)) return null;
05311:         if (lat < -90 || lat > 90 || lng < -180 || lng > 180) return null;
05312: 
05313:         return { latitude: lat, longitude: lng };
05314:     }

05316:     async function stationFetchWithTimeout(url, options = {}, timeoutMs = 10000) {
05317:         const controller = new AbortController();
05318:         STATION_STATE.activeControllers.add(controller);
05319:         const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);
05320: 
05321:         try {
05322:             return await fetch(url, {
05323:                 ...options,
05324:                 signal: controller.signal
05325:             });
05326:         } finally {
05327:             clearTimeout(timer);
05328:             STATION_STATE.activeControllers.delete(controller);
05329:         }
05330:     }

05332:     function extractStationArea(address) {
05333:         const input = String(address || '').trim();
05334:         const postcodeRegex = /\b(GIR\s?0AA|(?:[A-PR-UWYZ][0-9][0-9A-HJKPSTUW]?|[A-PR-UWYZ][A-HK-Y][0-9][0-9ABEHMNPRV-Y]?)\s?[0-9][ABD-HJLNP-UW-Z]{2})\b/i;
05335:         const match = input.match(postcodeRegex);
05336: 
05337:         if (!match || match.index == null) {
05338:             return { postcode: '', afterPostcode: '', area: '' };
05339:         }
05340: 
05341:         const postcode = match[1].toUpperCase().replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
05342:         const afterPostcode = input
05343:             .slice(match.index + match[0].length)
05344:             .replace(/^[\s,;:\-]+/, '')
05345:             .trim();
05346: 
05347:         const parts = afterPostcode
05348:             .split(',')
05349:             .map(part => part.trim())
05350:             .filter(Boolean);
05351: 
05352:         const countryOnly = /^(UNITED KINGDOM|UK|SCOTLAND|ENGLAND|WALES|NORTHERN IRELAND)$/i;
05353:         while (parts.length > 1 && countryOnly.test(parts[parts.length - 1])) parts.pop();
05354: 
05355:         return {
05356:             postcode,
05357:             afterPostcode,
05358:             area: parts.at(-1) || afterPostcode
05359:         };
05360:     }
05361: 
05362:     function formatStationArea(area) {
05363:         return String(area || '')
05364:             .replace(/\band\b/gi, '&')
05365:             .replace(/\s*&\s*/g, ' & ')
05366:             .replace(/^[\s,;:\-]+|[\s,;:\-]+$/g, '')
05367:             .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
05368:             .trim()
05369:             .toUpperCase();
05370:     }
05371: 
05372:     function buildStationName(formattedArea, suffix, currentName) {
05373:         const sequence = getExistingStationSequence(currentName, suffix);
05374:         return `${formattedArea}${suffix}${sequence}`;
05375:     }
05376: 
05377:     function getExistingStationSequence(currentName, suffix) {
05378:         if (!suffix) return '';
05379:         const escapedSuffix = suffix.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
05380:         const match = String(currentName || '').trim().match(new RegExp(`${escapedSuffix}\\s*(\\d+)$`, 'i'));
05381:         return match ? match[1] : '';
05382:     }

05389:     async function waitForStationNamingIframe(stationHref, preferredIframe = null, maximumAttempts = 80) {
05390:         for (let i = 0; i < maximumAttempts; i++) {
05391:             if (STATION_STATE.stopped) return null;
05392: 
05393:             const candidates = [
05394:                 preferredIframe,
05395:                 ...document.querySelectorAll(
05396:                     'iframe.lightbox_iframe[src^="/buildings/"], ' +
05397:                     'iframe#lssmv4-redesign-lightbox-iframe, ' +
05398:                     'iframe.mc-namer-managed-station-iframe'
05399:                 )
05400:             ].filter(Boolean);
05401: 
05402:             for (const iframe of candidates.reverse()) {
05403:                 try {
05404:                     const doc = iframe.contentDocument;
05405:                     const currentPath = iframe.contentWindow?.location?.pathname || iframe.getAttribute('src') || '';
05406:                     if (doc && (currentPath.startsWith(stationHref) || doc.querySelector(`a[href="${stationHref}/move"], form[action="${stationHref}"], form[action="${stationHref}/move_do"]`))) {
05407:                         return iframe;
05408:                     }
05409:                 } catch (error) {
05410:                     stationDebug(`Iframe access retry: ${error?.message || error}`);
05411:                 }
05412:             }
05413: 
05414:             await sleep(250);
05415:         }
05416: 
05417:         return null;
05418:     }
05419: 
05420:     async function waitForStationNamingDocument(iframe, predicate, label) {
05421:         for (let i = 0; i < 100; i++) {
05422:             if (STATION_STATE.stopped) return null;
05423: 
05424:             try {
05425:                 const doc = iframe?.contentDocument;
05426:                 if (doc && doc.readyState !== 'loading' && predicate(doc)) {
05427:                     stationDebug(`${label} ready at ${iframe.contentWindow?.location?.pathname || iframe.getAttribute('src') || 'unknown path'}`);
05428:                     return doc;
05429:                 }
05430:             } catch (error) {
05431:                 stationDebug(`${label} wait retry: ${error?.message || error}`);
05432:             }
05433: 
05434:             await sleep(200);
05435:         }
05436: 
05437:         return null;
05438:     }

05440:     async function verifyStationNameSaved(stationHref, expectedName) {
05441:         for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= 14; attempt++) {
05442:             if (STATION_STATE.stopped) return false;
05443: 
05444:             await sleep(attempt === 1 ? 500 : 350);
05445: 
05446:             try {
05447:                 const response = await stationFetchWithTimeout(`${stationHref}/edit`, {
05448:                     credentials: 'same-origin',
05449:                     cache: 'no-store'
05450:                 }, 10000);
05451: 
05452:                 if (!response.ok) {
05453:                     stationDebug(`Save verification HTTP ${response.status}, attempt ${attempt}.`);
05454:                     continue;
05455:                 }
05456: 
05457:                 const html = await response.text();
05458:                 const doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(html, 'text/html');
05459:                 const savedName = String(doc.querySelector('#building_name')?.value || '').trim();
05460:                 stationDebug(`Save verification attempt ${attempt}: "${savedName}"`);
05461: 
05462:                 if (savedName === expectedName) return true;
05463:             } catch (error) {
05464:                 stationDebug(`Save verification attempt ${attempt} failed: ${error?.message || error}`);
05465:             }
05466:         }
05467: 
05468:         return false;
05469:     }

05471:     function updateStationListName(href, newName) {
05472:         const entry = findStationOverviewEntry(href);

05474:         const container = entry?.container || getStationOverviewContainer(link);
05475:         if (!link) return;

05477:         const sameBuildingLinks = container
05478:             ? [...container.querySelectorAll('a[href]')]
05479:                 .filter(candidate => normaliseStationOverviewHref(candidate.getAttribute('href')) === entry.href)
05480:             : [link];
05481:         const nameLink = sameBuildingLinks.find(candidate => {
05482:             const label = cleanText(candidate.textContent);
05483:             return label && !isGenericStationActionText(label);
05484:         });
05485:         const nameNode = nameLink || container?.querySelector(
05486:             '[data-building-name], .building_list_title, .building-list-title, ' +
05487:             '.building_name, .building-name, .panel-heading, .card-title'
05488:         );

05491:             nameNode.textContent = newName;
05492:             nameNode.setAttribute?.('data-building-name', newName);
05493:         }
05494:         if (container) container.setAttribute('search_attribute', newName);
05495:     }

05572:     function getPersonnelStationScopeLabel() {
05573:         const service = getPersonnelServiceDefinition();
05574:         const stationTypes = getPersonnelProfileStationTypes();
05575: 
05576:         if (service === PERSONNEL_SERVICE_DEFINITIONS.medical) return 'ambulance stations';
05577:         if (stationTypes.length === 1 && stationTypes[0] === 'AIR') return 'police aviation stations';
05578:         if (stationTypes.length === 1 && stationTypes[0] === 'EOD') return 'EOD / bomb disposal stations';
05579:         if (service === PERSONNEL_SERVICE_DEFINITIONS.police) return 'relevant Police stations';
05580:         return `relevant ${service.label} stations`;

05583:     function refreshPersonnelStations() {
05584:         if (!isLivePersonnelProfile()) {
05585:             const service = getPersonnelServiceDefinition();
05586:             const profile = getPersonnelProfileDefinition();
05587:             personnelLog(`${service.label} > ${profile.label} is currently a UI preview only. No station scan was started.`, 'error');

05593:             personnelLog('A naming tool is currently running. Stop it before refreshing Personnel Assignment.', 'error');
05594:             setPersonnelUiValue('status', 'Blocked by naming tool');
05595:             return;
05596:         }
05597: 
05598:         if (PERSONNEL_STATE.running) {
05599:             personnelLog('Stop Personnel Assignment before refreshing the station list.', 'error');
05600:             return;

05604:         const scopeLabel = getPersonnelStationScopeLabel();
05605:         setPersonnelUiValue('status', `Refreshing ${scopeLabel}...`);
05606:         personnelLog(`Refreshing ${scopeLabel}...`, 'info');

05609: 
05610:         PERSONNEL_STATION_LINK_BY_HREF.clear();

05616:             .map((entry, index) => ({
05617:                 index,
05618:                 href: entry.href,
05619:                 buildingId: entry.buildingId,
05620:                 displayName: entry.displayName,
05621:                 buildingTypeId: entry.buildingTypeId,

05623:                     STATION_BUILDING_TYPE_INFO[entry.buildingTypeId]?.stationType ||

05627:

05631:

05633:         if (PERSONNEL_STATE.filteredStations.length) {

05636:         }

05690:     function getPersonnelTrainingRegistryStats(registry = readPersonnelTrainingRegistry()) {
05691:         const vehicles = registry?.vehicles && typeof registry.vehicles === 'object'
05692:             ? Object.values(registry.vehicles)
05693:             : [];
05694:         const updatedAt = vehicles.reduce(
05695:             (latest, entry) => Math.max(latest, Number(entry?.updatedAt || 0)),

05750: 
05751:     function normaliseImportedPersonnelTrainingRegistry(candidate) {
05752:         const source = candidate?.registry && typeof candidate.registry === 'object' ? candidate.registry : candidate;
05753:         if (!source || typeof source !== 'object' || Array.isArray(source)) {
05754:             throw new Error('The selected file does not contain a Personnel Register object.');
05755:         }
05756:         const schemaVersion = source.schemaVersion == null
05757:             ? PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_SCHEMA_VERSION
05758:             : Number(source.schemaVersion);
05759:         if (schemaVersion !== PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_SCHEMA_VERSION) {
05760:             throw new Error(`Unsupported Personnel Register schema version: ${schemaVersion}.`);
05761:         }
05762:         if (!source.vehicles || typeof source.vehicles !== 'object' || Array.isArray(source.vehicles)) {
05763:             throw new Error('The selected file does not contain a valid vehicles register.');
05764:         }
05765:         const entries = Object.entries(source.vehicles);
05766:         if (entries.length > PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_MAX_VEHICLES) {
05767:             throw new Error(`The file contains ${entries.length} vehicles; the supported maximum is ${PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_MAX_VEHICLES}.`);
05768:         }
05769:         const vehicles = Object.create(null);
05770:         entries.forEach(([rawVehicleId, rawEntry]) => {
05771:             const vehicleId = String(rawVehicleId || '').slice(0, 80);
05772:             if (isUnsafePersonnelTrainingRegistryKey(vehicleId)) throw new Error('The file contains an invalid vehicle identifier.');
05773:             if (!rawEntry || typeof rawEntry !== 'object' || Array.isArray(rawEntry)) {
05774:                 throw new Error(`Vehicle ${vehicleId} does not contain a valid register entry.`);
05775:             }
05776:             const safeCount = value => {
05777:                 const count = Number(value || 0);
05778:                 return Number.isFinite(count) && count >= 0 ? Math.floor(count) : 0;
05779:             };
05780:             const safeTimestamp = value => {
05781:                 const timestamp = Number(value || 0);
05782:                 return Number.isFinite(timestamp) && timestamp >= 0 ? timestamp : 0;
05783:             };
05784:             const assignedTrainingProfiles = Array.isArray(rawEntry.assignedTrainingProfiles)
05785:                 ? rawEntry.assignedTrainingProfiles.slice(0, 250).map(profile => {
05786:                     return Array.from(new Set(
05787:                         (Array.isArray(profile) ? profile : [])
05788:                             .slice(0, 80)
05789:                             .map(value => String(value || '').slice(0, 120))
05790:                             .filter(Boolean)
05791:                     )).sort();
05792:                 })
05793:                 : [];
05794:             vehicles[vehicleId] = {
05795:                 vehicleId,
05796:                 vehicleName: String(rawEntry.vehicleName || '').slice(0, 500),
05797:                 vehicleTypeId: String(rawEntry.vehicleTypeId || '').slice(0, 80),
05798:                 stationName: String(rawEntry.stationName || '').slice(0, 500),
05799:                 stationHref: String(rawEntry.stationHref || '').slice(0, 1000),
05800:                 assignedPersonnelCount: safeCount(rawEntry.assignedPersonnelCount),
05801:                 assignmentScanComplete: rawEntry.assignmentScanComplete === true,
05802:                 personnelRowsSeen: safeCount(rawEntry.personnelRowsSeen),
05803:                 trainingCounts: normalisePersonnelTrainingRegistryCountMap(rawEntry.trainingCounts),
05804:                 trainingCombinationCounts: normalisePersonnelTrainingRegistryCountMap(rawEntry.trainingCombinationCounts),
05805:                 assignedTrainingProfiles,
05806:                 trainingProfilesComplete:
05807:                     rawEntry.trainingProfilesComplete === true &&
05808:                     Array.isArray(rawEntry.assignedTrainingProfiles),
05809:                 updatedAt: safeTimestamp(rawEntry.updatedAt),
05810:                 source: String(rawEntry.source || '').slice(0, 250)
05811:             };
05812:         });
05813:         const safeRegistryTimestamp = value => {
05814:             const timestamp = Number(value || 0);
05815:             return Number.isFinite(timestamp) && timestamp >= 0 ? timestamp : 0;
05816:         };
05817:         return {
05818:             schemaVersion: PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_SCHEMA_VERSION,
05819:             sourceVersion: String(source.sourceVersion || '').slice(0, 80),
05820:             updatedAt: safeRegistryTimestamp(source.updatedAt),
05821:             lastPrunedAt: safeRegistryTimestamp(source.lastPrunedAt),
05822:             vehicles
05823:         };
05824:     }

05826:     function downloadPersonnelTrainingRegistryJson(filename, payload) {
05827:         const blob = new Blob([`${JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2)}\n`], { type: 'application/json;charset=utf-8' });
05828:         const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
05829:         const link = document.createElement('a');
05830:         link.href = url;
05831:         link.download = filename;
05832:         link.hidden = true;
05833:         document.body.appendChild(link);
05834:         link.click();
05835:         link.remove();
05836:         setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000);
05837:     }

05863:     async function importPersonnelTrainingRegistry(file) {
05864:         if (!file) return;
05865:         if (PERSONNEL_STATE.running) {
05866:             window.alert('Finish or stop the active Personnel operation before importing a register.');
05867:             return;
05868:         }
05869:         if (Number(file.size || 0) > PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_IMPORT_MAX_BYTES) {
05870:             window.alert('Personnel Register import failed: the selected file is larger than 10 MB.');
05871:             return;
05872:         }
05873:         let registry;
05874:         try {
05875:             const parsed = JSON.parse(await file.text());
05876:             if (parsed?.format && parsed.format !== PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_EXPORT_FORMAT) {
05877:                 throw new Error(`Unsupported export format: ${String(parsed.format).slice(0, 120)}`);
05878:             }
05879:             registry = normaliseImportedPersonnelTrainingRegistry(parsed);
05880:         } catch (error) {
05881:             personnelLog(`Personnel Register import failed: ${error?.message || error}`, 'error');
05882:             window.alert(`Personnel Register import failed: ${error?.message || error}`);
05883:             return;
05884:         }
05885:         const importedCount = Object.keys(registry.vehicles).length;
05886:         if (!importedCount) {
05887:             window.alert('The selected file contains an empty Personnel Register. Nothing was imported.');
05888:             return;
05889:         }
05890:         const existingCount = getPersonnelTrainingRegistryStats().count;
05891:         if (!window.confirm(
05892:             `Import ${importedCount.toLocaleString('en-GB')} vehicle register entries?\n\n` +
05893:             `This replaces the ${existingCount.toLocaleString('en-GB')} entries currently stored in this browser.`
05894:         )) return;
05895:         try {
05896:             if (PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_FLUSH_TIMER != null) {
05897:                 clearTimeout(PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_FLUSH_TIMER);
05898:                 PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_FLUSH_TIMER = null;
05899:             }
05900:             localStorage.setItem(PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(registry));
05901:             PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_CACHE = registry;
05902:             PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_DIRTY = false;
05903:             PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_PENDING_EVENT = null;
05904:         } catch (error) {
05905:             personnelLog(`Personnel Register import could not be saved: ${error?.message || error}`, 'error');
05906:             window.alert(`Personnel Register could not be saved: ${error?.message || error}`);
05907:             return;
05908:         }
05909:         dispatchPersonnelTrainingRegistryUpdate({ imported: true, vehicleCount: importedCount, updatedAt: Date.now() });
05910:         updatePersonnelTrainingRegistryStatus();
05911:         personnelLog(`Personnel Register imported: ${importedCount} vehicle(s). Existing browser register replaced.`, 'done');
05912:         setPersonnelUiValue('status', 'Personnel register imported');

06033:                 !resolvedVehicleId ||
06034:                 !stationVehicleIds.has(resolvedVehicleId)
06035:             ) {

06037:             }

06051:                         ).map(String).filter(Boolean)
06052:                     )

06072:     function isPersonnelRegistryVehicleSnapshotReusable({
06073:         vehicle,
06074:         station,
06075:         existingEntry,
06076:         expectedProfiles,
06077:         snapshotSafe,
06078:         fullVerify
06079:     }) {
06080:         if (fullVerify || !snapshotSafe || !existingEntry) return false;
06081:         if (
06082:             !existingEntry.assignmentScanComplete ||
06083:             !existingEntry.trainingProfilesComplete
06084:         ) {
06085:             return false;
06086:         }
06087:         if (
06088:             !String(existingEntry.source || '').startsWith(
06089:                 'personnel-register-exact-'
06090:             )
06091:         ) {
06092:             return false;
06093:         }
06094:         const verifiedAt = Number(existingEntry.updatedAt || 0);
06095:         if (
06096:             !Number.isFinite(verifiedAt) ||
06097:             verifiedAt <= 0 ||
06098:             Date.now() - verifiedAt > PERSONNEL_REGISTER_REVERIFY_AGE_MS
06099:         ) {
06100:             return false;
06101:         }
06102:         if (
06103:             String(existingEntry.vehicleId || '') !== String(vehicle.vehicleId) ||
06104:             String(existingEntry.vehicleTypeId || '') !== String(vehicle.vehicleTypeId || '') ||
06105:             String(existingEntry.stationHref || '') !== String(station.href || '')
06106:         ) {
06107:             return false;
06108:         }
06109:         if (
06110:             Number(existingEntry.assignedPersonnelCount || 0) !==
06111:             expectedProfiles.length
06112:         ) {
06113:             return false;
06114:         }
06115:         return getPersonnelRegisterCanonicalProfiles(
06116:             existingEntry.assignedTrainingProfiles
06117:         ) === getPersonnelRegisterCanonicalProfiles(expectedProfiles);
06118:     }

06120:     function mergePersonnelRegisterEvidence(target, person) {
06121:         const personnelId = String(person?.personnelId || '');
06122:         if (!personnelId) return;
06123:         const existing = target.get(personnelId);
06124:         const newHasExactBinding = Boolean(person?.assignedVehicleId);
06125:         const existingHasExactBinding = Boolean(existing?.assignedVehicleId);
06126:         if (
06127:             !existing ||
06128:             (person.assignedHere && !existing.assignedHere) ||
06129:             (newHasExactBinding && !existingHasExactBinding)
06130:         ) {
06131:             target.set(personnelId, person);
06132:         }
06133:     }

06135:     async function runPersonnelRegisterVehicleVerificationPool({
06136:         vehicles,
06137:         mergedPersonnel,
06138:         station
06139:     }) {
06140:         const queue = Array.isArray(vehicles) ? vehicles.slice() : [];
06141:         const verifiedVehicles = [];
06142:         let exactPagesRead = 0;
06143:         let failedVehicles = 0;
06144:         const failedVehicleIds = [];
06145:         let cursor = 0;
06146:         let nextLaunchAt = 0;
06147:         let launchChain = Promise.resolve();
06148:         const waitForLaunchSlot = () => {
06149:             const scheduled = launchChain.then(async () => {
06150:                 const delay = Math.max(0, nextLaunchAt - Date.now());
06151:                 if (delay > 0) {
06152:                     await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, delay));
06153:                 }
06154:                 nextLaunchAt = Date.now() +
06155:                     PERSONNEL_REGISTER_LAUNCH_GAP_MS;
06156:             });
06157:             launchChain = scheduled.catch(() => {});
06158:             return scheduled;
06159:         };
06160:         const concurrency = Math.max(
06161:             1,
06162:             Math.min(
06163:                 isIosSafariWebsite() ? 2 : PERSONNEL_REGISTER_MAX_CONCURRENCY,
06164:                 queue.length || 1
06165:             )
06166:         );
06167: 
06168:         const worker = async () => {
06169:             while (true) {
06170:                 if (PERSONNEL_STATE.stopped) return;
06171:                 await waitIfPersonnelPaused();
06172:                 if (PERSONNEL_STATE.stopped) return;
06173:                 const index = cursor;
06174:                 cursor += 1;
06175:                 if (index >= queue.length) return;
06176:                 const vehicle = queue[index];
06177:                 setPersonnelUiValue(
06178:                     'vehicle',
06179:                     `${vehicle.name || vehicle.vehicleId} (${index + 1}/${queue.length})`
06180:                 );
06181:                 setPersonnelUiValue('status', 'Reading exact vehicle assignments');
06182: 
06183:                 let parsed = null;
06184:                 let finalError = null;
06185:                 for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 2; attempt++) {
06186:                     try {
06187:                         await waitForLaunchSlot();
06188:                         const assignmentPage = await personnelFetchDocument(
06189:                             vehicle.assignmentHref,
06190:                             14000
06191:                         );
06192:                         if (!assignmentPage.doc.querySelector('#personal_table')) {
06193:                             throw new Error(
06194:                                 'Personnel table was not present on the assignment page.'
06195:                             );
06196:                         }
06197:                         parsed = parseVehicleAssignmentPage(
06198:                             assignmentPage.doc,
06199:                             vehicle.vehicleId
06200:                         );
06201:                         break;
06202:                     } catch (error) {
06203:                         finalError = error;
06204:                         if (attempt === 0 && !PERSONNEL_STATE.stopped) {
06205:                             await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 900));
06206:                         }
06207:                     }
06208:                 }
06209: 
06210:                 if (!parsed) {
06211:                     failedVehicles += 1;
06212:                     failedVehicleIds.push(String(vehicle.vehicleId || ''));
06213:                     personnelLog(
06214:                         `Exact vehicle scan failed for ${vehicle.name || vehicle.vehicleId}: ${finalError?.message || finalError}`,
06215:                         'error'
06216:                     );
06217:                     continue;
06218:                 }
06219: 
06220:                 parsed.rows.forEach(person => {
06221:                     mergePersonnelRegisterEvidence(mergedPersonnel, person);
06222:                 });
06223:                 verifiedVehicles.push(vehicle);
06224:                 exactPagesRead += 1;
06225:             }
06226:         };
06227: 
06228:         await Promise.all(
06229:             Array.from({ length: concurrency }, () => worker())
06230:         );
06231: 
06232:         return {
06233:             station,
06234:             verifiedVehicles,
06235:             exactPagesRead,
06236:             failedVehicles,
06237:             failedVehicleIds
06238:         };
06239:     }

07365:     function publishPersonnelVehicleTrainingRegistry({
07366:         station,
07367:         vehicles,
07368:         personnel,
07369:         source = 'scan',
07370:         pruneMissingVehicles = true
07371:     }) {
07372:         const stationVehicles = Array.isArray(vehicles)
07373:             ? vehicles.filter(vehicle => vehicle?.vehicleId)

07376:         if (!stationVehicles.length) return 0;
07377: 
07378:         const registry = readPersonnelTrainingRegistry();

07381:             stationVehicles.map(vehicle => String(vehicle.vehicleId))
07382:         );
07383:         const processedTypeIds = new Set(
07384:             stationVehicles.map(vehicle => String(vehicle.vehicleTypeId || ''))
07385:         );
07386: 
07387:         if (pruneMissingVehicles) {
07388:             Object.entries(registry.vehicles).forEach(([vehicleId, entry]) => {
07389:                 if (String(entry?.stationHref || '') !== String(station?.href || '')) return;
07390:                 if (!processedTypeIds.has(String(entry?.vehicleTypeId || ''))) return;

07392:             });
07393:         }
07394:

07405:                 getPersonnelAssignedTrainingProfiles(assignedPersonnel);
07406:

07408:                 vehicleId,

07410:                 vehicleTypeId: String(vehicle.vehicleTypeId || ''),
07411:                 stationName: String(station?.displayName || station?.stationName || ''),
07412:                 stationHref: String(station?.href || ''),
07413:                 assignedPersonnelCount: assignedPersonnel.length,
07414:                 assignmentScanComplete: true,
07415:                 personnelRowsSeen: Number.isFinite(vehicle.assignmentPersonnelRowsSeen)
07416:                     ? Math.max(0, vehicle.assignmentPersonnelRowsSeen)
07417:                     : (Array.isArray(personnel) ? personnel.length : 0),
07418:                 trainingCounts: countPersonnelTrainingCodes(assignedPersonnel),
07419:                 trainingCombinationCounts: countPersonnelTrainingCombinations(assignedPersonnel),
07420:                 assignedTrainingProfiles,
07421:                 trainingProfilesComplete: exactVehicleProfileScan,
07422:                 updatedAt: now,
07423:                 source: String(source)
07424:             };
07425:         });
07426: 
07427:         registry.schemaVersion = PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_SCHEMA_VERSION;
07428:         registry.sourceVersion = PERSONNEL_VERSION;
07429:         registry.updatedAt = now;
07430:         PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_DIRTY = true;
07431:         PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_PENDING_EVENT = {
07432:             stationName: station?.displayName || '',
07433:             vehicleCount: stationVehicles.length,
07434:             updatedAt: now
07435:         };

07440:

07447:     function getPersonnelAssignmentIndex(personnel) {

07478:     function getPersonnelAssignedToVehicle(personnel, vehicle) {
07479:         if (!Array.isArray(personnel) || !vehicle) return [];

07485:         const byName = vehicleNameKey ? (index.byVehicleName.get(vehicleNameKey) || []) : [];
07486:

07488:         if (!byName.length || byName === byId) return byId;
07489:

07494:             if (!seen.has(personnelId)) {
07495:                 seen.add(personnelId);
07496:                 result.push(person);
07497:             }
07498:         });
07499:         return result;
07500:     }

06791:     async function processPersonnelQueue() {
06792:         try {
06793:             while (
06794:                 PERSONNEL_STATE.stationIndex <
06795:                 PERSONNEL_STATE.queue.length

06804:                         PERSONNEL_STATE.stationIndex

06810:                 setPersonnelUiValue(

06813:                 );

06815:                     'current',

06817:                 );

06821:                 );

06824:                     'Scanning station'
06825:                 );
06826: 
06827:                 personnelLog(
06828:                     `Station ${displayPosition}/${PERSONNEL_STATE.queue.length}: ${station.displayName}`,
06829:                     'station'
06830:                 );

06832:                 let completedReport;
06833: 
06834:                 try {
06835:                     completedReport =
06836:                         await processOnePersonnelStation(
06837:                             station
06838:                         );
06839: 
06840:                     if (isPersonnelPerStationTargetMode()) {
06841:                         const stationTarget =
06842:                             PERSONNEL_STATE.unitsRequired;
06843: 
06844:                         const stationFulfilled =
06845:                             Math.min(
06846:                                 stationTarget,
06847:                                 Number(
06848:                                     completedReport.fulfilledUnits
06849:                                     || 0
06850:                                 )
06851:                             );

06854:                             stationTarget;

06856:                             stationFulfilled;

06858:                             stationFulfilled >= stationTarget;

06860:                             Math.max(
06861:                                 0,

06894:                         completedReport.assignmentShortfall;

06899:                                 completedReport.stationTargetMet
06900:                                     ? ` | target PASSED ${completedReport.stationTargetFulfilled}/${completedReport.stationTarget}`
06901:                                     : ` | target SHORT ${completedReport.stationTargetFulfilled}/${completedReport.stationTarget}`
06902:                             )

06907:                         (
06908:                             completedReport.trainingShortfall
06909:                             || completedReport.assignmentShortfall
06910:                             || (
06911:                                 isPersonnelPerStationTargetMode()
06912:                                 && !completedReport.stationTargetMet

06914:                         )

07079:             }
07080:         }
07081:     }
07082: 
07083:     function prunePersonnelTrainingRegistry(
07084:         registry,
07085:         now = Date.now(),
07086:         maximumVehicles = PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_MAX_VEHICLES
07087:     ) {
07088:         const sourceVehicles = registry?.vehicles && typeof registry.vehicles === 'object'
07089:             ? registry.vehicles
07090:             : {};
07091:         const cutoff = now - PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_MAX_AGE_MS;
07092:         const validEntries = Object.entries(sourceVehicles)
07093:             .filter(([vehicleId, entry]) => {
07094:                 const updatedAt = Number(entry?.updatedAt || 0);
07095:                 return Boolean(
07096:                     vehicleId
07097:                     && entry
07098:                     && typeof entry === 'object'
07099:                     && Number.isFinite(updatedAt)
07100:                     && updatedAt >= cutoff
07101:                 );
07102:             })
07103:             .sort((left, right) => Number(right[1]?.updatedAt || 0) - Number(left[1]?.updatedAt || 0));
07104: 
07105:         const retainedEntries = validEntries.slice(0, Math.max(0, Number(maximumVehicles) || 0));
07106:         const vehicles = Object.fromEntries(retainedEntries);
07107:         const originalCount = Object.keys(sourceVehicles).length;
07108:         const retainedCount = retainedEntries.length;
07109: 
07110:         registry.vehicles = vehicles;
07111: 
07112:         return {
07113:             originalCount,
07114:             retainedCount,
07115:             removedCount: Math.max(0, originalCount - retainedCount)
07116:         };
07117:     }
07118: 
07119:     function savePersonnelTrainingRegistry(registry) {
07120:         const now = Date.now();
07121:         const sourceVehicles = registry?.vehicles && typeof registry.vehicles === 'object'
07122:             ? registry.vehicles
07123:             : {};
07124:         const sourceCount = Object.keys(sourceVehicles).length;
07125:         const lastPrunedAt = Number(registry?.lastPrunedAt || 0);
07126:         const shouldPrune = sourceCount > PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_MAX_VEHICLES
07127:             || !Number.isFinite(lastPrunedAt)
07128:             || now - lastPrunedAt >= PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_PRUNE_INTERVAL_MS;
07129:         const primaryPrune = shouldPrune
07130:             ? prunePersonnelTrainingRegistry(
07131:                 registry,
07132:                 now,
07133:                 PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_MAX_VEHICLES
07134:             )
07135:             : {
07136:                 originalCount: sourceCount,
07137:                 retainedCount: sourceCount,
07138:                 removedCount: 0
07139:             };
07140: 
07141:         if (shouldPrune) registry.lastPrunedAt = now;
07142: 
07143:         try {
07144:             localStorage.setItem(
07145:                 PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_STORAGE_KEY,
07146:                 JSON.stringify(registry)
07147:             );
07148: 
07149:             return {
07150:                 saved: true,
07151:                 pruned: primaryPrune.removedCount,
07152:                 retained: primaryPrune.retainedCount,
07153:                 quotaRetry: false
07154:             };
07155:         } catch (error) {
07156:             const isQuotaError = error?.name === 'QuotaExceededError'
07157:                 || error?.name === 'NS_ERROR_DOM_QUOTA_REACHED'
07158:                 || Number(error?.code) === 22
07159:                 || Number(error?.code) === 1014;
07160: 
07161:             if (!isQuotaError) throw error;
07162: 
07163:             const retryPrune = prunePersonnelTrainingRegistry(
07164:                 registry,
07165:                 now,
07166:                 PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_QUOTA_RETRY_MAX_VEHICLES
07167:             );
07168:             registry.lastPrunedAt = now;
07169: 
07170:             localStorage.setItem(
07171:                 PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_STORAGE_KEY,
07172:                 JSON.stringify(registry)
07173:             );
07174: 
07175:             return {
07176:                 saved: true,
07177:                 pruned: Math.max(primaryPrune.removedCount, retryPrune.removedCount),
07178:                 retained: retryPrune.retainedCount,
07179:                 quotaRetry: true
07180:             };
07181:         }
07182:     }
07183:

07194:     function readPersonnelTrainingRegistry() {
07195:         if (PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_CACHE) {
07196:             return PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_CACHE;
07197:         }
07198: 
07199:         const emptyRegistry = createEmptyPersonnelTrainingRegistry();

07201:         try {

07203:                 localStorage.getItem(PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_STORAGE_KEY) || 'null'
07204:             );
07205: 
07206:             if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== 'object' || typeof parsed.vehicles !== 'object') {

07208:                 return PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_CACHE;
07209:             }

07212:                 ...emptyRegistry,
07213:                 ...parsed,
07214:                 vehicles: { ...parsed.vehicles }
07215:             };

07218:             PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_CACHE.lastPrunedAt = Date.now();

07220:         } catch (_error) {
07221:             PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_CACHE = emptyRegistry;
07222:             return PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_CACHE;
07223:         }

07237:     function flushPersonnelTrainingRegistry(silent = false) {
07238:         if (PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_FLUSH_TIMER != null) {
07239:             clearTimeout(PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_FLUSH_TIMER);
07240:             PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_FLUSH_TIMER = null;
07241:         }
07242: 
07243:         if (!PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_DIRTY || !PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_CACHE) {
07244:             return { saved: false, pruned: 0, retained: 0, quotaRetry: false };
07245:         }
07246: 
07247:         try {
07248:             const registrySave = savePersonnelTrainingRegistry(PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_CACHE);
07249:             PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_DIRTY = false;
07250: 
07251:             const pendingEvent = PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_PENDING_EVENT;
07252:             PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_PENDING_EVENT = null;
07253:             dispatchPersonnelTrainingRegistryUpdate(pendingEvent);
07254:             updatePersonnelTrainingRegistryStatus();
07255: 
07256:             if (!silent) {
07257:                 personnelDebug(
07258:                     `Shared training register saved: retained=${registrySave.retained} | pruned=${registrySave.pruned}${registrySave.quotaRetry ? ' | quota retry used' : ''}`
07259:                 );
07260:             }
07261: 
07262:             return registrySave;
07263:         } catch (error) {
07264:             if (!silent) {
07265:                 personnelLog(`Shared training register could not be saved: ${error?.message || error}`, 'error');
07266:             }
07267:             return { saved: false, pruned: 0, retained: 0, quotaRetry: false, error };
07268:         }
07269:     }

07682:     function sortPersonnelCandidatesForRule(candidates, rule) {
07683:         return candidates.slice().sort((left, right) => {
07684:             const leftReserved = rule.preferWithout.some(code => left.trainingCodes.includes(code)) ? 1 : 0;
07685:             const rightReserved = rule.preferWithout.some(code => right.trainingCodes.includes(code)) ? 1 : 0;
07686:             if (leftReserved !== rightReserved) return leftReserved - rightReserved;
07687: 
07688:             const extraDifference = left.trainingCodes.length - right.trainingCodes.length;
07689:             if (extraDifference) return extraDifference;
07690:             return String(left.name || '').localeCompare(String(right.name || ''), 'en-GB', { numeric: true });
07691:         });
07692:     }

05162:     function getBuildingRecordFromCache(cache, buildingId) {
05163:         if (!cache) return null;
05164: 
05165:         const numericId = Number(buildingId);
05166:         const keys = [buildingId, Number.isFinite(numericId) ? numericId : buildingId];
05167: 
05168:         if (typeof cache.get === 'function') {
05169:             for (const key of keys) {
05170:                 const value = cache.get(key);
05171:                 if (value) return value;
05172:             }
05173:         }
05174: 
05175:         if (Array.isArray(cache)) {
05176:             return cache.find(item => String(item?.id ?? item?.building_id ?? '') === String(buildingId)) || null;
05177:         }
05178: 
05179:         if (typeof cache === 'object') {
05180:             for (const key of keys) {
05181:                 if (cache[key]) return cache[key];
05182:             }
05183: 
05184:             return Object.values(cache).find(item => String(item?.id ?? item?.building_id ?? '') === String(buildingId)) || null;
05185:         }
05186: 
05187:         return null;
05188:     }

05234:     function extractBuildingRecords(payload) {
05235:         if (!payload) return [];
05236:         if (Array.isArray(payload)) return payload;
05237:         if (Array.isArray(payload.buildings)) return payload.buildings;
05238:         if (Array.isArray(payload.result)) return payload.result;
05239:         if (Array.isArray(payload.data)) return payload.data;
05240: 
05241:         if (typeof payload === 'object') {
05242:             const values = Object.values(payload);
05243:             const directRecords = values.filter(value => value && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value));
05244:             if (directRecords.some(value => 'latitude' in value && 'longitude' in value)) return directRecords;
05245: 
05246:             for (const value of values) {
05247:                 if (Array.isArray(value)) return value;
05248:                 if (value && typeof value === 'object') {
05249:                     const nested = extractBuildingRecords(value);
05250:                     if (nested.length) return nested;
05251:                 }
05252:             }
05253:         }
05254: 
05255:         return [];
05256:     }

10020:     function cleanText(text) {
10021:         return String(text || '')
10022:             .replace(/🚑|🚒|🚓|🚁|🚤|🏥|🔥|🚨|☢️|🤿|🦺|🎖️|🚔|🚛|🧯|🚐|🚚|🚙|🚗|🚜|🚢|🛟|🎯|🏣|🧑‍🚒|🚌|🚒🪜|🚔🔫|🚔🥅|🚔🐎|⚠️|🛻|🟤|🛥️|✈️|🪜|📡|🛠️|🌊|🟡|🏔️|🐕|💣|❤️|💧|👷|🫧|1️⃣|2️⃣/g, '')
10023:             .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
10024:             .trim();
10025:     }
10026: 
10027:     function createCallsignBase(stationName) {
10028:         return cleanText(stationName)
10029:             .replace(/\s+AO Station$/i, '')
10030:             .replace(/\s+Ambulance Station$/i, '')
10031:             .replace(/\s+Fire Station$/i, '')
10032:             .replace(/\s+Police Station$/i, '')
10033:             .replace(/\s+Station$/i, '')
10034:             .trim();
10035:     }
10036: 
10037:     function detectStationType(name) {
10038:         const n = cleanText(name).toUpperCase();
10039: 
10040:         if (n.includes('AIRMED') || n.includes('AIR AMB')) return 'AIR';
10041:         if (n.includes('AIRPORT')) return 'AIRFIELD';
10042:         if (n.includes('BOMB') || n.includes('EOD')) return 'EOD';
10043:         if (n.includes('RECOVERY')) return 'RECOVERY';
10044:         if (n.includes('SAR')) return 'SAR';
10045:         if (n.includes('RNLI') || n.includes('-LB') || n.includes('LIFEBOAT')) return 'RNLI';
10046:         if (n.includes('-CG') || n.includes('COASTGUARD')) return 'COASTGUARD';
10047:         if (n.includes('-PS') || n.includes('POLICE')) return 'POLICE';
10048:         if (n.includes('-AS') || n.includes('AO STATION') || n.includes('AMBULANCE')) return 'AMBULANCE';
10049:         if (n.includes('-FS') || n.includes('FIRE')) return 'FIRE';
10050: 
10051:         return 'OTHER';
10052:     }
